In this work, we introduce \textsc{CarCrashNet}, a public high-fidelity open-source benchmark for data-driven structural crash simulation. \textsc{CarCrashNet} combines component-scale and full-vehicle simulations in a multi-modal format, including more than 14{,}000 bumper-beam pole-impact simulations with varying geometry, materials, and boundary conditions, together with 825 full-vehicle crash simulations built from three industry-standard vehicle models of increasing structural complexity: Dodge Neon, Toyota Yaris, and Chevrolet Silverado. To establish the reliability of the benchmark, we validate our open-source finite-element workflow based on OpenRadioss against both experimental crash data and the commercial solver Ansys LS-DYNA. We also introduce \textsc{CrashSolver}, a machine-learning model designed for full-vehicle crash prediction from high-resolution finite-element crash data. We further perform extensive benchmarking across the released datasets and evaluate \textsc{CrashSolver} against state-of-the-art geometric deep learning and transformer-based neural solvers. Our results position \textsc{CarCrashNet} as a foundation for reproducible research in structural simulation, crashworthiness modeling, and AI-driven virtual crash testing. The dataset is available at https://github.com/Mohamedelrefaie/CarCrashNet.

</details>


### [152] [Almost Sure Convergence Rates of Stochastic Approximation and Reinforcement Learning via a Poisson-Moreau Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07104)
*Xinyu Liu,Zixuan Xie,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对马尔可夫噪声下的随机逼近和强化学习算法，建立了几乎必然收敛速度的理论结果。对于幂律学习率（η∈(1/2,1)）和谐波学习率（O(n⁻¹)），分别获得了接近最优的收敛速度上界，核心创新在于将基于泊松方程的马尔可夫噪声修正与Moreau包络平滑相结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在马尔可夫噪声下建立随机逼近和强化学习算法的几乎必然收敛速度是一个基础性的理论挑战，现有理论尚不完善。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的Lyapunov漂移构造方法，将基于泊松方程的马尔可夫噪声修正与Moreau包络平滑技术相结合，用于处理压缩映射。

Result: 对于幂律学习率O(n⁻η)（η∈(1/2,1)），获得了任意接近o(n^{1-2η})的几乎必然收敛速度；对于谐波学习率O(n⁻¹)，获得了任意接近o(n⁻¹)的几乎必然收敛速度，接近i.i.d.噪声下的最优率O(n⁻¹log log n)。

Conclusion: 该工作为马尔可夫噪声下强化学习算法的收敛性分析提供了重要的理论进展，所提方法具有通用性。

Abstract: Establishing almost sure convergence rates for stochastic approximation and reinforcement learning under Markovian noise is a fundamental theoretical challenge. We make progress towards this challenge for a class of stochastic approximation algorithms whose expected updates are contractive, a setting that arises in many reinforcement learning algorithms such as $Q$-learning and linear temporal difference learning. Specifically, for a power-law learning rate $O(n^{-η})$ with $η\in (1/2, 1)$, we obtain an almost sure convergence rate arbitrarily close to $o(n^{1 - 2η})$. For a harmonic learning rate $O(n^{-1})$, we obtain an almost sure convergence rate arbitrarily close to $o(n^{-1})$, which we argue is a strong result because it is close to the optimal rate $O(n^{-1}\log\log n)$ given by the law of the iterated logarithm (for a special case of i.i.d. noise). Key to our analysis is a novel Lyapunov drift construction that applies a Poisson-equation based correction for Markovian noise to the well-established Moreau-envelope smoothing for the contractive mapping.

</details>


### [153] [Where to Spend Rollouts: Hit-Utility Optimal Rollout Allocation for Group-Based RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07114)
*Tao Wang,Shuo Li,Yan Sun,Dongsheng Ding,Edgar Dobriban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HORA方法，通过动态分配rollout预算替代GRPO的均匀分配，在数学推理任务中提升Pass@K性能，且无需额外学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等基于组的策略优化方法对每个提示分配固定数量的rollout，导致计算资源浪费：有些提示的采样组已饱和，而另一些提示需要更多样本来发现正确轨迹。

Method: 定义"命中效用"（hit utility）作为追加分配中至少一个rollout正确的后验概率，提出HORA（Hit-Utility Optimal Rollout Allocation）无学习分配策略，在每批中最大化总后验命中效用，自适应重新分配rollout预算，不改变下游奖励评估和优势估计器。

Result: 在四个数学推理基准和三种模型规模上，HORA在12个模型-基准配置中的10个上保持了相当的Pass@1并提升了Pass@K（1个平局，1个饱和例外），且与RLOO等其他组估计器兼容。

Conclusion: HORA是一种高效、无需学习的rollout分配策略，能有效提升计算效率；消融实验表明其使用的均匀先验与五种提示条件学习先验方法竞争力相当。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a central paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models. Group-based policy optimization methods, such as GRPO, typically allocate a fixed number of rollouts to every prompt. This uniform allocation can be inefficient: it over-allocates compute to prompts whose sampled groups are already saturated while under-exploring prompts for which additional samples may reveal useful correct trajectories. To address this limitation, we introduce hit utility, the posterior probability that at least one rollout in a proposed additional allocation for a prompt will be correct. Building on this notion, we propose Hit-Utility Optimal Rollout Allocation (HORA), a learning-free rollout allocation policy that maximizes total posterior hit utility within each allocation batch. HORA adaptively reallocates rollout budgets while leaving the downstream reward evaluation and group-based advantage estimator unchanged. Across four mathematical reasoning benchmarks and three model scales, HORA preserves comparable Pass@1 and improves Pass@K over compute-matched GRPO in ten of twelve model--benchmark configurations, with one tie and one saturated exception. It is also drop-in compatible with other group-based estimators such as RLOO. Ablation studies indicate that the uniform prior used by HORA is competitive with five prompt-conditioned learned-prior alternatives.

</details>


### [154] [Conformal-Style Quantile Analyses for Stochastic Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07115)
*Chengyu Du,Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ACP-UCB1算法，通过自适应共形预测估计上尾端点并融合UCB乐观奖励，在随机赌博机问题中实现对数级上分位数遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机赌博机算法通常以均值奖励为标准，但许多实际问题更关注上尾性能（即高分位点）。均值与上尾端点对臂的排序可能不一致，导致经典目标与需求不匹配。

Method: 提出ACP-UCB1策略，结合自适应共形估计（ACP）估计上尾端点与UCB型乐观奖励。通过奖励-分位数集中性、重计算得分分位数的扰动论证及自适应水平的确定性定位来控制端点估计。

Result: ACP-UCB1达到对数级上分位数遗憾，每个臂的遗憾界为O(log n / Δ_j^{ACP})。提供了与UCB1对比的度量特定遗憾分解，并通过数值实验验证性能提升。

Conclusion: ACP-UCB1有效解决了以均值标准与上尾性能目标之间的不匹配问题，在理论上实现了对数级遗憾界，实验表明其性能优于传统UCB1。

Abstract: Stochastic bandit algorithms are usually analyzed under a mean-reward criterion, yet many problems favor arms with strong upper-tail performance, which we study herein. For a fixed miscoverage level \(α\), the natural upper-tail target of arm \(j\) is the upper endpoint \(F_j^{-1}(1-α/2)\) of a central prediction interval. This target can rank arms differently from their means, creating a central mismatch with the classical bandit objective. To this end, we propose ACP-UCB1, a conformal-style policy that combines an adaptive conformal estimate of the upper endpoint with a UCB-type optimism bonus. The technical challenge is that the conformity scores used by ACP-UCB1 are recomputed from evolving empirical quantile estimates and evaluated at an adaptive level. We control this endpoint through reward-quantile concentration, a perturbation argument for recomputed score quantiles, and deterministic localization of the adaptive level. ACP-UCB1 achieves logarithmic upper-quantile regret with per-arm contribution \(O(\nicefrac{\log n}{Δ_j^{\mathrm{ACP}}})\). We also provide metric-specific regret decompositions comparing ACP-UCB1 with UCB1 and use numerical experiments to validate performance and improvement.

</details>


### [155] [When Symbol Names Should Not Matter: A Logistic Theory of Fresh-Symbol Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07120)
*Wenjie Guan,Jelena Bradic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer在模板任务中是否使用抽象符号推理，通过正则化核逻辑分类分析，揭示了理想模板级分类器与有限样本扰动之间的分解关系，并建立了基于碰撞图的边距转移保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer能否基于抽象符号(而非具体token名称)进行推理，并解释其在符号重命名不变性下的泛化能力。

Method: 采用Transformer核机制下的正则化核逻辑分类，通过构建有色碰撞图编码训练数据中的偶然token重叠，从理论上分解预测器为理想模板分类器与有限样本扰动项。

Result: 证明了新鲜符号分类的高概率边距转移保证，揭示了词汇量控制平均碰撞率，而碰撞几何决定理想分类边距是否保持。

Conclusion: 该扰动框架可扩展至抽象增强输入，提出边距-碰撞准则用于识别提示策略何时能改善新鲜符号泛化，并通过合成实验验证了正则化、样本量和核结构的作用。

Abstract: Template tasks have emerged as a clean testbed for asking whether transformers reason with abstract symbols rather than concrete token names. We study the fixed-label classification version of this problem, where train and test examples share latent templates but may use disjoint vocabularies. Unlike next-token prediction, the model need not emit unseen symbols; it must learn a decision rule invariant to symbol renaming. We analyze regularized kernel logistic classification in the transformer-kernel regime. Our main result decomposes the learned predictor into an ideal template-level classifier and a finite-sample perturbation caused by accidental token overlaps in the training data. We encode these overlaps by a colored collision graph and prove high-probability margin-transfer guarantees for fresh-symbol classification. This perspective extends template-based analyses to logistic classification and refines scalar diversity conditions: vocabulary size controls the average rate of collisions, but collision geometry controls whether the ideal classification margin is preserved. More broadly, the same perturbation framework applies to abstraction-augmented inputs, yielding a general margin-versus-collision criterion for identifying when prompting strategies improve fresh-symbol generalization. Synthetic template experiments illustrate the predicted roles of regularization, sample size, and transformer-kernel structure.

</details>


### [156] [Convergence and Emergence of In-Context Reinforcement Learning with Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07123)
*Zixuan Xie,Xinyu Liu,Rohan Chandra,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从理论上分析了Chain-of-Thought生成如何增强上下文强化学习能力，证明CoT过程等价于反复执行时序差分学习更新，并提供了有限样本收敛分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明CoT能增强ICRL能力，但缺乏理论解释，本文旨在从理论上理解CoT与ICRL的交互机制。

Method: 在策略评估设置下使用线性Transformer进行分析，证明特定Transformer参数下CoT生成等价于重复TD学习更新，并提供有限样本收敛分析以及预训练损失的全局极小化证明。

Result: CoT过程等价于重复执行TD学习更新；策略评估误差随CoT长度几何级下降，最终受限于上下文长度的统计下界；特定Transformer参数是预训练损失的全局极小化器。

Conclusion: 该研究为CoT增强ICRL提供了理论依据，揭示了其工作原理，有助于理解大语言模型的上下文学习能力。

Abstract: In-context reinforcement learning (ICRL) refers to the ability of RL agents to adapt to new tasks at inference time without parameter updates by conditioning on additional context. Recent empirical studies further demonstrate that Chain-of-Thought (CoT) generation can amplify this ICRL capability. This paper is the first to provide a theoretical understanding on how CoT interacts with ICRL. We conduct our analysis in a policy evaluation setup with linear Transformer. We prove that with specific Transformer parameters, the CoT generation process is equivalent to repeatedly executing temporal difference learning updates. Additionally, we provide finite sample convergence analysis showing that the policy evaluation error decreases geometrically with CoT length and eventually saturates at a statistical floor determined by the context length. We also prove that the desired Transformer parameters are a global minimizer of the pretraining loss, providing a theoretical understanding on the empirical emergence of those parameters.

</details>


### [157] [The Position Curse: LLMs Struggle to Locate the Last Few Items in a List](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07127)
*Zhanqi Zhang,Hua-Dong Xiong,Robert C. Wilson,Mikio Aoi,Marcelo G. Mattar,Li Ji-An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在长文本中查找特定信息表现优秀，但在短列表中检索最后几项时却表现糟糕，这种现象称为"位置诅咒"。研究发现后向检索能力显著弱于前向检索，LoRA微调虽能改善但远未饱和，对代码理解任务至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在"大海捞针"任务上表现接近饱和，但在短列表中检索末尾项时却频繁失败，这种矛盾亟待系统研究。

Method: 设计了两种互补查询任务（位置→项、项→位置），使用前/后向偏移指定位置；构建了位置聚焦训练数据集PosBench，采用LoRA微调进行实验。

Result: 后向检索性能显著落后于前向检索；LoRA微调能提升双向检索能力并可泛化到代码理解基准测试，但绝对性能仍远未饱和。

Conclusion: 基于位置的检索是LLM的关键能力缺陷，尤其在代码理解与编辑场景中，应成为未来预训练目标和模型设计的重要关注点。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) can find a needle in a haystack (locating a single relevant fact buried among hundreds of thousands of irrelevant tokens) with near-saturated accuracy, yet fail to retrieve the last few items in a short list. We call this failure the Position Curse. For instance, even in a two-line code snippet, Claude Opus 4.6 misidentifies the second-to-last line most of the time. To characterize this failure, we evaluated two complementary queries: given a position in a sequence (of letters or words), retrieve the corresponding item; and given an item, return its position. Each position is specified as a forward or backward offset from an anchor, either an endpoint of the list (its start or end) or another item in the list. Across both open-source and frontier closed-source models, backward retrieval substantially lags forward retrieval. To test whether this capability can be rescued by post-training, we constructed PosBench, a position-focused training dataset. LoRA fine-tuning improves both forward and backward retrieval and generalizes to a held-out code-understanding benchmark (PyIndex), yet absolute performance remains far from saturated. As LLM coding agents increasingly operate over large codebases where precise indexing becomes essential for code understanding and editing, position-based retrieval emerges as a key capability for future pretraining objectives and model design.

</details>


### [158] [Simple KNN-Based Outlier Detection Achieves Robust Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07130)
*Tianle Jiang,Yufa Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在最优聚类大小的实用假设下，简单地移除具有较大K近邻距离的点，即可将鲁棒k-Means问题转化为标准k-Means问题，并实现与先前工作相当的近似保证，且无需引入额外中心或丢弃多余离群点。实验表明，这种基于KNN的简单启发式方法在聚类成本和运行时间上优于或匹配多种复杂算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管鲁棒k-Means与离群点检测密切相关，但经典离群点检测启发式方法在鲁棒k-Means中的理论和实践效果尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在最优聚类大小的实用假设下，通过移除具有较大K近邻距离的点，将鲁棒k-Means问题简化为标准k-Means问题，并给出常数因子的近似保证。

Result: 该方法在不需要额外中心或丢弃多余离群点的情况下，实现了与先前工作相当的近似保证；在真实数据集上，聚类成本和运行时间优于或匹配多种更复杂的算法。

Conclusion: 简单的KNN启发式方法对鲁棒聚类非常有效，这为桥接离群点检测和聚类技术提供了新的机会。

Abstract: Being robust to the presence of outliers is crucial for applying clustering algorithms in practice. In the $\textit{robust $k$-Means}$ problem (i.e., $k$-Means with outliers), the goal is to remove $z$ outliers and minimize the $k$-Means cost on the remaining points. Despite the close connection between robust $k$-Means and outlier detection, both theoretical and empirical understanding of the effectiveness of $\textit{classic outlier detection heuristics}$ for robust $k$-Means remains limited. In this paper, we prove that under a practical assumption on the optimal cluster sizes, simply removing points with large $K$-Nearest-Neighbor distances achieves performance comparable to prior work in terms of approximation guarantees: it yields a constant-factor reduction from robust $k$-Means to standard $k$-Means, without introducing additional centers or discarding extra outliers, as is commonly required by existing approaches. Empirically, experiments on real-world datasets show that our method outperforms or matches several more sophisticated algorithms in terms of clustering cost and runtime. These results demonstrate that simple KNN-based heuristics can be surprisingly effective for robust clustering, highlighting new opportunities to bridge techniques from outlier detection and clustering.

</details>


### [159] [GAD in the Wild: Benchmarking Graph Anomaly Detection under Realistic Deployment Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07133)
*Jingjing Zhou,Shiyu Huang,Qing Qing,Zuquan Yuan,Huafei Huang,Ziqi Xu,Mingliang Hou,Xikun Zhang,Renqiang Luo,Ivan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个面向图异常检测（GAD）的多维度基准测试，系统评估了模型在百万级图、极端异常稀疏和缺失节点属性等实际部署挑战下的表现，发现现有模型在规模化、低异常率和属性缺失场景中性能严重下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GAD基准多局限于小规模、精心标注且异常比例相对平衡的数据集，导致学术评估与实际部署之间存在巨大差距。

Method: 从5个不同图数据（含两个原生工业级超370万节点数据集）衍生出受控基准变体，系统评估9种代表性GAD模型在百万级规模、极端异常稀疏（0.1%）和缺失节点属性三种挑战下的表现。

Result: (1) 多数GNN方法因内存需求无法扩展至百万节点；(2) 真实异常比例下检测性能骤降，常出现零召回率；(3) 重建模型对属性插补策略高度敏感。

Conclusion: 实验室环境下的优异表现不能保证生产环境中的鲁棒性，需开发面向大规模、不完美图数据的鲁棒可扩展GAD系统。

Abstract: Graph Anomaly Detection (GAD) is a critical task in graph machine learning with vital applications in financial fraud detection and social platform governance. However, existing GAD benchmarks are often restricted to small-scale, curated graphs with relatively balanced anomaly ratios, leaving a substantial gap between academic evaluation and real-world deployment. To bridge this gap, we present a multi-dimensional benchmark that systematically evaluates GAD models under three deployment-relevant challenges: million-scale graphs, extreme anomaly scarcity, and missing node attributes. We derive a family of controlled benchmark variants from five diverse graphs, including two native industrial-scale datasets with over 3.7 million nodes. Our extensive evaluation of nine representative GAD models reveals three major limitations: (1) most GNN-based methods fail to scale to million-node graphs due to prohibitive memory requirements; (2) detection performance drops sharply under realistic anomaly ratios (e.g., 0.1\%), often resulting in zero recall; and (3) reconstruction-based models are highly sensitive to attribute imputation strategies. Our findings suggest that strong performance in laboratory settings does not guarantee robustness in production environments. We release this benchmark and empirical evaluation as a diagnostic testbed to promote the development of robust and scalable GAD systems for large-scale, imperfect graphs encountered in practice. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Benchmark_GAD-E7A3.

</details>


### [160] [Adaptive Negative Reinforcement for LLM Reasoning:Dynamically Balancing Correction and Diversity in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07137)
*Yash Ingle,Jaival Chauhan,Ankit Yadav,Sudhakar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种改进的负样本强化学习方法（A-NSR和CW-NSR），通过自适应时间调度和基于置信度的加权惩罚，在数学推理任务上提升了LLM的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有负样本强化学习方法使用固定惩罚权重，无法区分错误类型的重要程度，且在训练过程中缺乏动态调整机制。

Method: 提出两种扩展：A-NSR使用时间依赖调度函数，在训练初期侧重纠错、后期进行精细调整；CW-NSR基于模型对错误路径的置信度分配不同惩罚权重，高置信度错误获得更大惩罚。

Result: 在MATH、AIME 2025、AMC23等困难推理数据集上，使用Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B架构进行评估，方法有效。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应和置信度加权的负样本强化方法能更精细地引导概率重分配，同时具备抗过拟合能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a highly effective method for improving the reasoning abilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). Recent research shows that Negative Sample Reinforcement (NSR) -- which focuses on penalizing incorrect steps rather than simply rewarding correct ones -- can match or even exceed the performance of more complex frameworks like PPO and GRPO across the entire Pass@k spectrum. However, current NSR techniques usually apply a fixed penalty throughout the training process and treat every incorrect response with the same weight.
  To address these limitations, we propose two extensions to the NSR framework: Adaptive Negative Sample Reinforcement. Rather than using a fixed update rule, A-NSR uses time-dependent scheduling functions. In the initial training phases, the system focuses heavily on correcting errors to stabilize the model. As training continues, it shifts toward more subtle and controlled updates. We also introduce Confidence-Weighted Negative Reinforcement, which operates on the principle that different mistakes carry different levels of importance. CW-NSR assigns specific penalty weights based on the model's normalized sequence likelihood. If the model is highly confident in a wrong path, it receives a larger penalty and for uncertain errors -- where the model is effectively exploring -- are penalized less strictly. Our formal analysis shows how these mechanisms govern token-level updates, allowing the model to leverage prior-guided probability redistribution while providing a natural defense against overfitting. We evaluated these methods on difficult reasoning datasets, including MATH, AIME 2025, and AMC23, using the Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B architecture.

</details>


### [161] [Regret-Oracle Complexity Tradeoffs in Agnostic Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07155)
*Idan Attias,Steve Hanneke,Arvind Ramaswami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于弱一致性预言机的自适应动态不可知到可学习约简方法，将预言机复杂度从双指数级降至O(T^{d_VC+1})，同时保持近最优期望遗憾，并形式化了遗憾-预言机复杂度之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的不可知在线学习依赖于Littlestone的标准最优算法(SOA)，但SOA即使在单轮计算上也难以处理。近期的预言机高效方法虽实现了近最优期望遗憾，但面临双指数级预言机复杂度的瓶颈。

Method: 提出一种自适应动态不可知到可学习约简方法，利用弱一致性预言机（仅判断标注数据集是否可学习）主动在线剪枝不可实现的标签序列，通过VC维(d_VC)约束动态维护的活动路径数量。

Result: 总查询复杂度降至O(T^{d_VC+1})，同时完美保持近最优期望遗憾；并实现了存储开销的降低。此外，给出了遗憾-预言机复杂度权衡的上界和下界，证明任何Q=o(√T)查询的学习器必须承受Ω(T/Q)的期望遗憾。

Conclusion: 该工作显著改进了不可知在线学习的预言机复杂度，从双指数降至多项式级别，同时建立了遗憾与查询预算之间的基本权衡关系。

Abstract: Agnostic online learning is classically solved via a reduction to the realizable setting, utilizing Littlestone's Standard Optimal Algorithm (SOA) as a base learner. However, the SOA is computationally intractable to execute even for a single round. To overcome this barrier, recent work in oracle-efficient online learning replaces the SOA with a realizable base learner that accesses the concept class exclusively through an offline empirical risk minimization (ERM) oracle. While such agnostic learners achieve near-optimal expected regret, they suffer from a doubly-exponential oracle complexity of $O\big(T^{2^{O(d_\mathrm{LD})}}\big)$, where $d_\mathrm{LD}$ is the Littlestone dimension and $T$ is the number of rounds. In this work, we significantly improve this oracle complexity while relying on an even weaker primitive: a weak-consistency oracle, which merely decides whether a given labeled dataset is realizable. At the core of our approach is an adaptive and dynamic agnostic-to-realizable reduction that actively prunes non-realizable label sequences on the fly. By using the VC dimension ($d_\mathrm{VC}$) to bound the number of dynamically maintained active paths, our algorithm reduces the total query complexity down to $O(T^{d_\mathrm{VC}+1})$ while perfectly preserving near-optimal expected regret. Crucially, this dynamic pruning also yields a memory reduction over the standard reduction. Furthermore, we formally quantify the regret--oracle complexity tradeoff, providing upper bounds that smoothly interpolate between restricted query budgets and attainable expected regret. We complement these with lower bounds proving that any learner restricted to $Q = o(\sqrt{T})$ queries must suffer an expected regret of $Ω(T/Q)$.

</details>


### [162] [Experience Sharing in Mutual Reinforcement Learning for Heterogeneous Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07244)
*Xiaoze Liu,Dhananjay Ram,Yuting Zhang,Zhaoyang Zhang,Wei Xia,Stefano Soatto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种互强化学习框架，用于异构LLM策略的并发RL后训练，通过共享经验交换、多工作负载资源分配和分词器异构层实现跨模型家族的经验共享，实验发现结果级共享在稳定性-支持权衡中占据有利位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后训练方法多为单一模型独立训练，缺乏异构模型间高效经验共享的机制，难以利用不同策略的互补优势。

Method: 提出Mutual Reinforcement Learning框架，包含共享经验交换(SEE)、多工作负载资源分配(MWRA)和分词器异构层(THL)；实例化三种探针：Peer Rollout Pooling(PRP)、Cross-Policy GRPO Advantage Sharing(XGRPO)和Success-Gated Transfer(SGT)。

Result: 通过上下文赌博机分析刻画了三者在稳定性-支持权衡中的结构位置：PRP付出密度比方差和THL残差成本，XGRPO保持策略支持但改变标量基线，SGT提供验证成功的得分方向。在评估范围内，结果级共享(SGT)占据该权衡的有利点。

Conclusion: 结果级共享（Success-Gated Transfer）在稳定性与策略支持之间取得了最佳平衡，是异构LLM策略并发RL后训练中经验共享的最优方式。

Abstract: We introduce Mutual Reinforcement Learning, a framework for concurrent RL post-training in which heterogeneous LLM policies exchange typed experience while keeping separate parameters, objectives, and tokenizers. The framework combines a Shared Experience Exchange (SEE), Multi-Worker Resource Allocation (MWRA), and a Tokenizer Heterogeneity Layer (THL) that retokenizes text and aligns token-level traces across incompatible vocabularies. This substrate makes the experience-sharing design question operational across model families. We instantiate three controlled probes on top of GRPO: data-level rollout sharing via Peer Rollout Pooling (PRP), value-level advantage sharing via Cross-Policy GRPO Advantage Sharing (XGRPO), and outcome-level success transfer via Success-Gated Transfer (SGT). A contextual-bandit analysis characterizes their structural positions on a stability-support trade-off: PRP pays density-ratio variance and THL residual costs, XGRPO preserves on-policy actor support while changing scalar baselines, and SGT supplies a rescue-set score direction toward verified peer successes. In the evaluated regime, outcome-level sharing occupies the favorable point of this trade-off.

</details>


### [163] [Cost-Ordered Feasibility for Multi-Armed Bandits with Cost Subsidy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07171)
*Ishank Juneja,Carlee Joe-Wong,Osman Yağan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带成本补贴的多臂老虎机（MAB-CS）问题，提出Cost-Ordered Feasibility（COF）算法，在已知成本、奖励约束相对于未知最优奖励的场景下，实现了更优的理论边界和实证表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MAB问题追求最大奖励，但实际应用中常需在满足最低奖励约束下最小化成本，现有研究对此关注不足。

Method: 提出COF算法，通过智能组合所有臂的样本来评估廉价臂的可行性，并建立实例相关的累积成本和奖励遗憾上下界。

Result: 推导了更严格的实例相关下界，证明了COF在理论遗憾上界上的定性优势，并在MovieLens、Goodreads数据集及合成数据上验证了其优于基线方法。

Conclusion: COF算法在带成本补贴的MAB问题上同时实现了理论保证和实证性能的提升。

Abstract: The classic multi-armed bandit (MAB) problem tackles the challenge of accruing maximum reward while making decisions under uncertainty. However, in applications, often the goal is to minimize cost subject to a constraint on the minimum permissible reward, an objective captured by multi-armed bandits with cost-subsidy (MAB-CS). Of interest to this paper is the setting where the quality (reward) constraint is specified relative to the unknown best reward and the cost of each arm is known. We characterize the expected sub-optimal samples required by any policy by proving instance-dependent lower bounds that offer new insight into the problem and are a strict generalization of prior bounds. Then, we propose an algorithm called Cost-Ordered Feasibility (COF) that leverages our insight and intelligently combine samples from all arms to gauge the feasibility of a cheap arm. Thereafter, we analyze COF to establish instance-dependent upper bounds on its expected cumulative cost and quality regret, i.e., relative to the cheapest feasible arm. Finally, we empirically validate the merits of COF, comparing it to baselines from the literature through extensive simulation experiments on the MovieLens and Goodreads datasets as well as representative synthetic instances. Not only does our paper develop qualitatively better theoretical regret upper bounds, but COF also convincingly demonstrates improved empirical performance.

</details>


### [164] [When Are Experts Misrouted? Counterfactual Routing Analysis in Mixture-of-Experts Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07260)
*Youngsik Yoon,Siwei Wang,Wei Chen,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现MoE语言模型的标准top-k路由在推理困难token时会选择次优的专家组合，而通过仅更新最后一层路由器即可显著提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MoE路由选择是否最优很少被直接评估，作者希望系统性地检验标准路由策略与同等计算量的替代路由方案之间的效果差异。

Method: 固定模型参数，对每个token比较标准路由与随机采样的同等计算量替代路由，以该token在验证推理轨迹中实际出现的下一个token概率作为效用评分；并在多个模型上进行验证。

Result: 标准路由在置信token上与路由效用对齐良好，但在驱动困难推理的脆弱token上信息量低；冻结模型内存在同等计算量但损失更低的替代路由未被选中；该现象在多个模型中一致出现。

Conclusion: 仅对最后一层路由器进行最小更新即可显著提升AIME和HMMT数学推理的pass@K，说明路由失败部分源于路由器自身的错误分配，而非仅由专家容量限制导致。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models route each token to a small subset of experts, but whether the routes selected by a trained top-$k$ router are good ones is rarely evaluated directly. Holding the model fixed, we compare each standard route against sampled equal-compute alternatives for the same token and score each by the next-token probability it assigns to the realized token in a verified reasoning trajectory. The result is sharply token-conditional: the standard router is well-aligned with route utility on confident tokens but uninformative on the fragile tokens that drive hard reasoning, where lower-loss equal-compute routes consistently exist inside the frozen model but are not selected. The same pattern holds across Qwen3-30B-A3B, GPT-OSS-20B, DeepSeek-V2-Lite, and OLMoE-1B-7B, and follows structurally from how standard top-$k$ training evaluates routing decisions: the language modeling loss scores only the executed route, and load balancing depends only on aggregate routing statistics. A minimal router-only update to the final-layer router, leaving every expert and every other router frozen, is sufficient to shift pass@K on AIME 2024+2025 and HMMT 2025 for both Qwen3-30B-A3B and GPT-OSS-20B, suggesting that at least part of the failure reflects router-reachable misallocation rather than expert capacity alone.

</details>


### [165] [Unsolvability Ceiling in Multi-LLM Routing: An Empirical Study of Evaluation Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07395)
*Saloni Garg,Amit Sagtani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 多LLM路由中的"不可解性"很大程度上源于评估伪影（裁判偏见、截断、格式不匹配），而非模型真实能力不足；标准路由器会退化为多数类预测，导致路由增益被高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由工作将路由增益归因于"不可解性天花板"，即查询池中没有任何模型能解决该查询。本文旨在验证这一假设的真实性。

Method: 大规模研究：206,000个查询-模型对，6个基准测试（MMLU, MedQA等），Gemma 4和Llama 3.1系列。使用LLM-as-a-judge和精确匹配评估，引入双裁判验证、精确匹配锚定、分解框架。

Result: 发现三种评估伪影：裁判偏袒冗长答案、固定预算截断、输出格式不匹配。这些伪影导致不可解性被高估。路由器训练信号扭曲，标准路由器退化为多数类预测（~79%最优），造成13-17百分点的机会成本。

Conclusion: 现有路由增益估计被大幅夸大，需要更可靠的评估协议（双裁判验证、精确匹配锚定、成本敏感目标）。

Abstract: Efficient routing across multiple LLMs enables cost-quality tradeoffs by directing queries to the cheapest capable model. Prior work attributes routing headroom to an "unsolvability ceiling", queries no model in the pool can solve. We present a large-scale study of multi-tier LLM routing with 206,000 query-model pairs across six benchmarks (MMLU, MedQA, HumanEval, MBPP, Alpaca, ShareGPT) using the Gemma 4 and Llama 3.1 families. Evaluating with both LLM-as-a-judge and exact-match metrics, we show that a substantial portion of reported unsolvability stems from evaluation artifacts: (i) systematic judge biases favoring verbosity over correctness, (ii) truncation under fixed generation budgets, and (iii) output format mismatches. Through dual-judge validation and exact-match grounding, we reduce measured unsolvability across tasks. We introduce a decomposition framework attributing failures to these artifacts, revealing consistent patterns across domains and model families. These artifacts also distort router training signals: standard routers collapse to majority-class prediction (~79% smallest-tier optimal), confirmed via random-feature and shuffled-label controls, incurring a 13-17 percentage point opportunity cost. We provide actionable recommendations including dual-judge validation, exact-match anchoring, and cost-sensitive objectives. Our findings suggest existing routing headroom estimates are substantially inflated, underscoring the need for reliable evaluation protocols in multi-LLM systems.

</details>


### [166] [ExpThink: Experience-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Chain-of-Thought Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07501)
*Tingcheng Bian,Yuzhe Zhang,Jing Jin,Jinchang Luo,MingQuan Cheng,Haiwei Wang,Wenyuan Jiang,Miaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ExpThink框架，通过经验引导奖励重塑和难度自适应优势机制，在保持甚至提升准确率的同时将推理响应长度减少77%，实现3倍于基准的准确率-效率比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型（LRMs）虽通过长链思维（CoT）推理表现优异，但存在过度token消耗和高推理延迟问题。现有RL压缩方法使用统一静态长度惩罚，忽略了模型能力动态变化和问题难度差异。

Method: 提出两个互补机制：1）经验引导奖励重塑——追踪每个问题最短正确解，采用三级奖励（简洁正确得满分、冗长正确得折扣分、错误得零分），阈值随模型进步自动收紧；2）难度自适应优势——用正确计数归一化代替标准偏差归一化，对困难问题放大梯度以保持准确率，对简单问题抑制梯度以鼓励简洁性。

Result: 在多个数学推理基准上，平均响应长度减少77%，同时准确率提升，准确率-效率比（准确率除以平均token数）比基线提高3倍。

Conclusion: ExpThink通过自适应机制实现了准确率优先、压缩次优的训练目标，在压缩推理长度和保持准确率两个维度均优于现有基于RL的压缩方法。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) achieve strong performance via extended chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, yet suffer from excessive token consumption and high inference latency. Existing reinforcement learning (RL) approaches for CoT compression rely on uniform, static length penalties that neglect model capability dynamics and problem-level difficulty variation. We propose \textbf{ExpThink}\xspace, an RL framework that addresses both dimensions through two complementary mechanisms. First, \emph{experience-guided reward shaping} tracks the shortest correct solution found so far for each problem and applies a three-tier reward: full credit for concise correct responses, discounted credit for verbose correct ones, and zero for incorrect ones. The threshold tightens automatically with model improvement, forming a self-evolving curriculum that requires no manual scheduling. Second, \emph{difficulty-adaptive advantage} replaces standard deviation normalization with correct-count normalization, yielding monotonically difficulty-scaled gradients that amplify learning on hard problems to preserve accuracy while suppressing gradients on easy ones to encourage brevity. Together, these mechanisms enforce an accuracy-first, compression-second training objective. Experiments on multiple mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that \textbf{ExpThink}\xspace reduces average response length by up to 77\% while simultaneously improving accuracy, achieving up to $3\times$ higher accuracy-efficiency ratio (accuracy divided by average token count) than the vanilla baseline and outperforming existing RL-based compression methods on both metrics.

</details>


### [167] [Coupling Models for One-Step Discrete Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07193)
*Fred Zhangzhi Peng,Avishek Joey Bose,Anru R. Zhang,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Coupling Models，一种一步式离散生成模型，通过直接学习离散序列与高斯潜变量之间的耦合，实现单步采样，在文本、生物序列和图像二值数据上显著优于现有一步式基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散生成模型多依赖自回归解码或迭代精炼，速度慢、效率低，而现有的蒸馏方法需要预训练多步采样器，且存在复杂的连续流和手动指定耦合等限制。

Method: 提出Coupling Models，直接学习离散序列与高斯潜变量之间的耦合，训练专用解码器来逆转这一耦合，实现单步生成，避免了复杂连续流和手动指定耦合。

Result: 在LM1B文本生成中困惑度降低33%，Fly Brain增强子设计FBD降低18%，MNIST-Binary FID降低46%，均超过现有最强一步式基线。

Conclusion: 一步式离散生成的效果强烈依赖于数据与噪声在解码前的耦合方式，Coupling Models证明了设计良好耦合机制的重要性。

Abstract: Generative modeling over discrete structures underpins applications across deep learning, from biological sequence design and code generation to large language models, yet generation often remains sequential, relying on autoregressive decoding or iterative refinement. In this work, we introduce Coupling Models(Coupling Models), a one-step discrete generative model that learns a direct coupling between discrete sequences and Gaussian latents. Unlike recent distillation methods that compress a pretrained multi-step sampler into a few steps, Coupling Model trains a purpose-built decoder to invert this coupling and generate samples in a single step. The model also avoids complex continuous flows over the simplex and hand-specified data-to-noise couplings. Empirically,Coupling Model improves the strongest one-step baselines in each domain: it reduces LM1B text-generation perplexity by 33% at its lowest-perplexity operating point, Fly Brain enhancer-design FBD by 18%, and MNIST-Binary FID by 46%. These results suggest that effective one-step discrete generation depends strongly on how data and noise are coupled before decoding. Code is available at https://github.com/pengzhangzhi/Coupling-Models.

</details>


### [168] [Arrow: A Foundation Model for Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07204)
*Ryan Thompson,He Zhao,Daniel M. Steinberg,Edwin V. Bonilla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Arrow，一个基于Transformer的零样本因果发现基础模型，通过在合成数据上预训练，能快速准确地从观测表格数据中发现有向无环图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法通常需要针对每个新数据集重新训练或进行大量计算，缺乏一个可复用、高效的零样本解决方案。

Method: Arrow将有向无环图分解为无向骨架和拓扑顺序，使用Transformer架构对变量进行上下文化处理，预测骨架边概率和节点顺序分数，并通过端到端可微的有向边复合似然进行监督训练。

Result: 在分布内外的合成、半合成及真实数据集上，Arrow匹配或超越了现有因果发现方法，且推理成本显著低于竞品。

Conclusion: 大规模多样化合成数据的预训练可以产生快速、准确且可复用的零样本因果发现模型。

Abstract: We introduce Arrow, a foundation model for zero-shot causal discovery on observational tabular data. Arrow factorizes a directed acyclic graph into an undirected skeleton and a topological order, guaranteeing acyclicity by construction. Given a new dataset, it uses a transformer-based architecture to contextualize variables within and across observations, then predicts skeleton edge probabilities and node order scores that together define a graph. Arrow is trained in a supervised fashion on synthetic datasets with ground-truth graphs, using an end-to-end differentiable directed edge composite likelihood induced by the skeleton-order factorization. The training distribution spans diverse graph families, functional forms, noise models, and dataset shapes. Across in- and out-of-distribution synthetic, semi-synthetic, and real datasets, Arrow matches or outperforms existing causal discovery methods at substantially lower inference cost than competitive alternatives. Our results demonstrate that large-scale pretraining on diverse synthetic data can yield zero-shot causal discovery models that are fast, accurate, and reusable on new datasets.

</details>


### [169] [HARMONY: Bridging the Personalization-Generalization Gap by Mitigating Representation Skew in Heterogeneous Split Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07211)
*Jiseok Youn,You Rim Choi,Goodsol Lee,Sangtae Ha,Hyung-Sin Kim,Saewoong Bahk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HARMONY是首个支持异构客户端架构的混合分割联邦学习框架，通过元学习模拟多样化特征提取器，并利用服务器端对比学习对齐特征，有效缓解表示偏移问题，在保持低延迟的同时显著提升了测试准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合SFL在客户端架构异构场景下存在表示偏移问题——不同客户端的定制化特征提取器提取的特征无法在共享空间中对齐，导致负责OOD预测的服务器模型性能严重下降。

Method: 1) 修改元学习以模拟不同参数和架构的多样化特征提取器，并学习个性化；2) 在服务器端进行对比学习来对齐提取的特征，既不牺牲客户端个性化，也不共享原始标签。

Result: 在多个数据集和模型族上，相比现有方法，HARMONY在无OOD场景下测试准确率提升最高43.0%，在有OOD场景下提升最高28.3%，同时保持可接受的延迟。

Conclusion: HARMONY有效解决了异构客户端架构下混合SFL的表示偏移问题，在准确率和效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Mobile devices face diverse resource constraints and non-IID data class distributions, requiring fast on-device inference for local in-distribution (ID) classes and on-demand remote support for client-specific out-of-distribution (OOD) classes. Hybrid split federated learning (Hybrid SFL) couples personalized client-side front ends (supporting early exit) with a generalized server-side backend for fallback inference, balancing accuracy and cost. However, under client architectural heterogeneity, the existing hybrid SFL suffers from representation skew, where features from customized extractors fail to align in the shared space, leading to a sharp degradation in the server model responsible for OOD prediction. We propose HARMONY, the first hybrid SFL framework to support heterogeneous client architectures. HARMONY modifies meta-learning to simulate diverse extractors across parameters and architectures, and to learn to personalize. To mitigate representation skew, HARMONY conducts server-side contrastive learning to align extracted features, neither sacrificing clients' personalization nor sharing raw labels. Compared to the state of the art across multiple datasets and model families, HARMONY improves test accuracy by up to 43.0%/28.3% without/with OOD, respectively, while maintaining acceptable latency.

</details>


### [170] [Same Brain, Different Prediction: How Preprocessing Choices Undermine EEG Decoding Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07212)
*Dengzhe Hou,Zihao Wu,Lingyu Jiang,Zirui Li,Fangzhou Lin,Kazunori D. Yamada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究发现EEG深度学习模型的预测结果在不同预处理流程下极不稳定，最高42%的试次级预测会翻转，并提出了三种工具来量化、分解和降低这种不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG深度学习模型通常只在一个未报告的单一预处理流程下训练和评估，忽略了预处理选择对模型预测稳定性的影响，标准不确定性方法无法量化这种因预处理变化带来的变异性。

Method: 将预处理选择形式化为反事实干预空间；使用Walsh-Hadamard分解对2^7种预处理组合进行可加性分析；提出预处理不确定性（PU）作为试次级诊断指标；采用归一化自适应PGI（NA-PGI）图结构正则化器来缓解不稳定性。

Result: 跨六个数据集（四种范式），仅改变预处理流程就导致最多42%的试次级预测翻转；敏感性在二元干预设计下近似可加；PU捕捉到了与模型置信度互补的不确定性维度。

Conclusion: EEG预测对预处理选择高度敏感，NA-PGI作为利用预处理干预组合结构的正则化方法可在特定条件下缓解这一问题，PU提供了新的不确定性量化维度。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is a cornerstone of brain-computer interfaces and clinical neuroscience, yet deep learning models are typically trained and evaluated under a single, unreported preprocessing pipeline. We formalize preprocessing choices as a counterfactual intervention space and show that EEG predictions are surprisingly unstable under this space: across six datasets spanning four paradigms, up to 42% of trial-level predictions flip when only the preprocessing changes, a variability that standard uncertainty methods do not explicitly quantify because they condition on a fixed preprocessing pipeline. We provide three tools to make this instability measurable, decomposable, and reducible. First, a Walsh-Hadamard decomposition of the 2^7 pipeline space reveals that sensitivity is near-additive in practice under the binary intervention design, enabling efficient step-by-step optimization. Second, we introduce Preprocessing Uncertainty (PU), a per-trial diagnostic that captures a dimension of instability complementary to model-based confidence. Third, we study Normalized Adaptive PGI (NA-PGI), a graph-structured regularizer that exploits the compositional structure of preprocessing interventions as one mitigation strategy with clear scope conditions.

</details>


### [171] [Improved Model-based Reinforcement Learning with Smooth Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07218)
*Kun Long,Yuqiang Li,Xianyi Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于核平滑的在线强化学习方法，通过引入伯恩斯坦风格探索奖励，在利普希茨连续假设下改进了有限时域设定中的遗憾界，特别是在时域依赖方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习理论多依赖于低秩MDP的结构性假设，而核平滑方法作为替代范式虽有潜力，但其遗憾界在时域依赖上不够理想。本文旨在通过伯恩斯坦风格探索奖励改进这一不足。

Method: 采用核平滑模型估计转移动态，并引入伯恩斯坦风格的探索奖励；同时推导了一个新的紧的伯恩斯坦型鞅集中不等式作为理论工具。

Result: 提出的方法在利普希茨连续假设下，取得了比现有最优结果更好的遗憾界，尤其在时域依赖方面有显著改进。

Conclusion: 核平滑方法与伯恩斯坦风格奖励的结合能有效提升在线强化学习的样本效率，新的鞅集中不等式也具有独立的理论价值。

Abstract: For continuous state-action space scenarios, classical reinforcement learning (RL) theory predominantly focuses on low-rank Markov decision processes (MDPs), which provide sample-efficient guarantees at the expense of restrictive structural assumptions. Kernel smoothing model-based approaches offer a promising alternative paradigm that instead leverages the smoothness of the MDP and employs non-parametric kernel smoothing estimates of transition dynamics. This paper proposes a new kernel-smoothing model-based approach for online reinforcement learning in finite-horizon settings under Lipschitz continuity assumptions on the MDP. By incorporating a Bernstein-style exploration bonus into the kernel smoothing framework, our method achieves a regret bound which improves upon the state-of-the-art regret bound in its dependence on the horizon. The theoretical advancement relies on a delicate analysis of the synergy between Bernstein-style bonuses and kernel smoothing, where a new tight Bernstein-type concentration inequality for martingales may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [172] [Trajectory as the Teacher: Few-Step Discrete Flow Matching via Energy-Navigated Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07924)
*Amin Karimi Monsefi,Dominic Culver,Nikhil Bhendawade,Manuel R. Ciosici,Yizhe Zhang,Irina Belousova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TS-DFM方法，通过用轻量级能量罗盘引导离散流匹配中的中间步骤，替换盲目随机跳跃，使得学生模型在8步推理时比1024步教师模型困惑度降低32%，速度提升128倍，且性能优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散流匹配的蒸馏方法中，学生模型性能不佳通常被归因于容量不足，但本文认为真正的瓶颈在于训练轨迹本身——中间步骤的盲目随机跳跃中，早期的一个糟糕决策会向后传播，而学生却必须模仿这种有缺陷的轨迹。

Method: TS-DFM用轻量级能量罗盘（energy compass）在训练阶段的每个中间步骤评估候选延续，选择最连贯的序列，从而用引导式导航替代盲目的随机跳跃。该引导仅在训练阶段使用，推理阶段不增加额外成本。

Result: 在1.7亿参数的语言模型上，8步训练的TS-DFM学生模型困惑度比1024步教师模型低32%，推理速度快128倍。该提升在多种源分布和三种不同规模的评估器下均保持一致。TS-DFM在所有对比的离散生成基线中取得了最佳困惑度，包括那些使用6倍数据或5倍大模型训练的方法。

Conclusion: 离散流匹配蒸馏的性能瓶颈并非学生模型容量不足，而是训练轨迹质量。通过改进轨迹质量，即使轻量级学生模型也能超越大规模教师模型，实现高质量且高效的文本生成。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching generates text by iteratively transforming noise tokens into coherent language, but may require hundreds of forward passes. Distillation uses the multi-step trajectory to train a student to reproduce the process in a few steps. When the student underperforms, the usual explanation is insufficient capacity. We argue the opposite: the trajectory is the bottleneck, not the student. Each training trajectory is built through a chain of blind stochastic jumps with no evaluation of sequence quality; a single bad decision at an early midpoint propagates through subsequent steps, yet the student must imitate the result. Trajectory-Shaped Discrete Flow Matching (TS-DFM) replaces these blind jumps with guided navigation: a lightweight energy compass evaluates candidate continuations at each midpoint, selecting the most coherent. All shaping is training-only; inference cost is unchanged. On 170M-parameter language modeling, the shaped student at 8 steps achieves 32% lower perplexity than the 1,024-step teacher while being 128x faster, with gains consistent across source distributions and three evaluators of increasing scale. TS-DFM achieves the best perplexity of any discrete-generation baseline we compare against, including methods trained on 6x more data or using 5x larger models.

</details>


### [173] [On the Robustness of Distribution Support under Diffusion Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07220)
*Ruijia Cao,Yuchen Wu,Nisha Chadramoorthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过建立“支持鲁棒性”理论属性，解释了扩散引导（Diffusion Guidance）为何能持续生成高质量、高保真样本，证明了在精确得分函数下，引导扩散过程几乎总能生成接近目标支持区域的样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散引导在经验上取得了巨大成功，但其理论性质尚未被充分理解，尤其是为何能一致生成高质量样本的原因不明。

Method: 提出“支持鲁棒性”（robustness of support）概念，从理论上分析引导扩散过程在精确得分函数下的样本生成行为，覆盖DDIM和DDPM两种模型，并适用于多种指数积分器离散化方案。

Result: 证明了在精确得分函数条件下，引导扩散过程几乎总能生成靠近目标支持区域的样本，即具有支持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作为扩散引导生成物理意义合理、结构合理样本提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: Diffusion guidance is a powerful technique that enables controllable and high-fidelity sample generation with diffusion models. At a high level, it modifies the score function by incorporating a guidance term that steers the generative process toward a desired condition. Despite its empirical success, the theoretical properties of diffusion guidance remain largely unexplored, and it is not well understood why it consistently produces high-quality samples.
  In this work, we explain the effectiveness of diffusion guidance by establishing a \emph{robustness of support} property. Specifically, we show that, given exact access to the score functions, guided diffusion processes almost always generate samples that remain close to the target support. This property is particularly desirable, as samples that lie off the support are often structurally implausible and may adversely affect downstream tasks. Our analysis covers both Denoising Diffusion Implicit Models (DDIM) and Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models (DDPM), and applies to a wide range of discretization schemes induced by exponential integrators. Our results provide a rigorous foundation for understanding why diffusion guidance produces physically meaningful and structurally plausible samples.

</details>


### [174] [Position: Mechanistic Interpretability Must Disclose Identification Assumptions for Causal Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08012)
*Zezheng Lin,Fengming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇论文发现机械可解释性研究普遍缺乏因果识别假设的明确陈述，验证指标常被错误地当作因果支持。作者提出了一项披露规范，要求研究明确声明因果主张、识别策略和假设敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性论文越来越多地使用因果词汇（如电路、中介、因果抽象、单义性），但缺乏相应的识别假设，导致因果主张的严谨性不足。

Method: 对10篇论文进行有目的审计，并在n=30样本上开展双编码员人工审计，验证识别假设缺失和验证指标替代因果支持的普遍性。

Result: 所有被审计论文均缺乏专门的识别假设部分；验证指标（忠实性、完整性、单义性、对齐性、消融效应）被报告为因果支持，而未说明使其具备识别力的假设；双编码员审计结果复现了主要发现方向。

Conclusion: 验证不等于识别。论文提出披露规范：声明主张是否为因果的、指明识别策略、列举假设、强调至少一个假设、说明假设不成立时结论如何变化。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability papers increasingly use causal vocabulary: circuits, mediators, causal abstraction, monosemanticity. Such claims require explicit identification assumptions. A purposive audit of 10 papers across four methodological strands finds no dedicated identification-assumptions section and a recurring pattern: validation metrics such as faithfulness, completeness, monosemanticity, alignment, or ablation effects are reported as causal support without stating the assumptions that make them identifying. A two-human-coder audit on $n=30$ reproduces the direction of the main finding: dedicated identification sections are absent, and validation-metric substitution is common, though exact Dim B/D counts are coding-rule sensitive. The paper proposes a disclosure norm: state whether the claim is causal, name the identification strategy, enumerate assumptions, stress at least one, and explain how conclusions shift if assumptions fail. Validation is not identification.

</details>


### [175] [Don't Learn the Shape: Forecasting Periodic Time Series by Rank-1 Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07222)
*Takato Honda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FLAIR方法，证明对于高秩1、多周期的时间序列预测，简单方法（如周期平均）优于复杂模型，额外灵活性只会增加估计噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究周期性时间序列预测到底需要多少参数，挑战"越复杂越好"的直觉，验证简单方法是否已被低估。

Method: 将小时级电力数据重塑为24行×天数矩阵，利用秩-1近似（每日形状×每日水平），测试8种替代方法（傅里叶、EWMA、James-Stein、秩-r SVD等），最终提出FLAIR：单个SVD+GCV平均Ridge。

Result: 所有8种复杂方法均未显著优于简单基线（冻结的K=2周期平均），两种甚至显著更差。FLAIR在GIFT-Eval上relMASE 0.838（与PatchTST的0.849相当），仅28个标量参数，单核22分钟完成。

Conclusion: 在高秩1、多周期的场景下，额外灵活性是估计噪声，简单封闭形式的周期平均方法足够有效，无需GPU、预训练或任务调参。

Abstract: How few parameters do we really need to forecast a periodic time series? An hourly electricity series, reshaped as a 24-row matrix with one column per day, is approximately rank-1: a daily shape modulated by a daily level (median centered rank-1 energy 0.82 on GIFT-Eval). Should we learn the shape? Smoothing, shrinkage, and low-rank fits all seem like obvious upgrades over the simple average of the last K=2 cycles. On all 97 GIFT-Eval configurations, we tested 8 such alternatives (e.g., Fourier, EWMA, James-Stein, rank-r SVD): none significantly beats the frozen baseline under Holm correction; two are significantly worse. The resulting method, FLAIR, is (a) Effective: matches PatchTST on aggregate GIFT-Eval (relMASE 0.838 vs 0.849); (b) Compact: 28 scalars for hourly, 57 for weekly; (c) Fast: 22 minutes on one CPU core of a MacBook Pro; (d) Closed-form & Hands-Off: one SVD per period candidate, GCV-averaged Ridge, no GPU, no pre-training, no per-task tuning. In the high-rank-1, many-cycle regime, extra flexibility is estimation noise.

</details>


### [176] [Modulated learning for private and distributed regression with just a single sample per client device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07233)
*Praneeth Vepakomma,Amirhossein Reisizadeh,Samuel Horváth,Munther Dahleh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对每客户端仅有一个样本的联邦学习场景的隐私保护方法，通过单次加噪变换和共享处理后的表示来学习全局模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标准联邦学习中，每个客户端只有单个样本时，局部更新效果极差，加上每轮隐私噪声后更加不可用，现有方法无法有效解决这一问题。

Method: 每个客户端对单个样本施加一次精心校准的噪声扰动，生成变换后的表示并发送给服务器；服务器聚合这些表示，得到无偏梯度更新，期望上等同于非私有的中心化梯度。

Result: 该方法使得仅持有单样本的设备能够协作学习准确的隐私保护模型，且梯度更新在期望上与非私有的中心化梯度一致。

Conclusion: 不同于传统联邦学习传输模型系数，该工作传输隐私变换后的数据表示，使数据极度有限的设备也能有效参与联邦学习，同时保护隐私。

Abstract: This work focuses on the question of learning from a large number of devices with each device holding only a single sample of data. Several real-world applications exist to this one sample per client setup up including learning from fitness trackers, data/app usage aggregators, body-worn sensing devices, and daily event monitors to name a few. When a client has only one sample, the standard federated learning paradigm breaks down as a local update based on that single point is far from being useful, especially in the earlier rounds for estimation of the model coefficients. This utility is further weakened by the privacy-inducing noise applied at every round. This work caters to this problem to enable such clients to collaboratively contribute to effectively learn a global model without leaking the privacy of their data. The proposed approach injects a single, carefully calibrated noisy perturbation to transform the sample at each client, followed by a post-processed representation which is shared with the server. These representations aggregated at the server are processed to obtain an unbiased gradient update that in expectation matches the non-private centralized gradient while preserving data privacy. This approach is different than traditional private federated learning, where the communication payloads involve model coefficients as opposed to privately transformed data samples. This method enables devices with extremely limited data to collaborate and learn accurate, privacy-preserving models without requiring large local datasets or sacrificing individual privacy.

</details>


### [177] [Sample Complexity of Stochastic Optimization with Integer Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07239)
*Hongyu Cheng,Yinghao Zheng,Marco Molinaro,Amitabh Basu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机整数优化的样本复杂度，发现其与连续优化相比，有时需要更多样本，有时需要更少，取决于目标和约束的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解整数随机优化与连续随机优化在样本复杂度上的本质差异，揭示结构如何影响统计复杂性。

Method: 通过理论分析，分别针对Lipschitz目标（ℓ∞球和ℓ2球子集）以及强凸光滑目标，推导并比较整数优化与连续优化的样本复杂度上下界。

Result: 1. ℓ∞球上：混合整数非线性非凸优化与线性约束优化的样本复杂度相同。2. ℓ2球上：整数优化在特定条件下样本复杂度严格低于连续优化。3. 强凸光滑目标：整数优化需要Ω(1/ε²)样本，而连续优化仅需O(1/ε)。

Conclusion: 整数优化的样本复杂度并非总是高于连续优化，其复杂性高度依赖于问题结构（如范数类型和目标光滑性）。

Abstract: We establish sample complexity results for stochastic optimization over the integers, especially with a view to understand the complexity with respect to the corresponding continuous optimization problem. We show that integer optimization can sometimes require strictly more samples and sometimes strictly smaller number of samples, depending on the structure of the objective and constraints. 1. For Lipschitz objectives over subsets of the $\ell_\infty$ ball, the statistical complexity of general stochastic mixed-integer, nonlinear, nonconvex optimization is exactly the same as stochastic linear optimization with just bound constraints. 2. For Lipschitz objectives over subsets of the $\ell_2$ ball, we show that integer optimization can require strictly *smaller* sample size compared to the continuous setting in a certain regime. To get to this result, we also establish tight sample complexity results for nonconvex continuous stochastic optimization which, to the best of our knowledge, do not appear in prior work. 3. For strongly convex, smooth objectives, integer optimization has high statistical complexity compared to the continuous setting. In particular, we show that integer optimization requires $Ω(1/ε^2)$ samples to report an $ε$-approximate solution, compared to the well-known $O(1/ε)$ sample complexity from the continuous optimization literature.

</details>


### [178] [PerCaM-Health: Personalized Dynamic Causal Graphs for Healthcare Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07267)
*Elahe Khatibi,Ziyu Wang,Saba A. Farahani,Di Huang,Hung Cao,Ramesh Jain,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PerCaM-Health 提出了一种从纵向健康数据中学习个性化动态因果图的框架，通过知识引导的群体图与患者特定证据结合，实现个性化因果推理与反事实查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间因果发现方法在个性化医疗中面临两难：群体模型稳定但缺乏个性化，单患者模型因数据稀疏、噪声大、非平稳而不可靠，存在群体因果建模与患者特定时变机制之间的鸿沟。

Method: 先学习知识引导的群体时间图，再通过患者特定时间证据和滚动窗口更新进行保守自适应演化，生成可解释的图序列，并耦合时间结构方程实现患者级反事实查询。

Result: 在半合成动态健康基准上，PerCaM-Health 在图恢复、动态边追踪和干预方向准确性方面均优于群体级、单患者级和非个性化时间基线方法。

Conclusion: 联合建模个性化和时间演化能够获得更可靠的因果结构，从而支持更准确的患者级干预推理。

Abstract: Personalized healthcare decisions require reasoning about how physiological and behavioral variables influence an individual patient over time. Existing temporal causal discovery methods are poorly matched to this setting: cohort-level models provide stable but non-personalized structures, while per-patient discovery is unreliable because individual trajectories are short, noisy, irregular, and non-stationary. This creates a fundamental gap between population-level causal modeling and the patient-specific, time-varying mechanisms needed for intervention reasoning. We introduce PerCaM-Health, a framework for learning personalized dynamic causal graphs from longitudinal health data. The framework learns a knowledge-guided population temporal graph, then conservatively adapts and evolves it using patient-specific temporal evidence and rolling-window updates, producing interpretable and auditable graph sequences. By coupling these graphs with temporal structural equations, the framework enables patient-level counterfactual queries, such as estimating short-horizon outcome changes under hypothetical behavioral interventions. Experiments on a semi-synthetic dynamic health benchmark show that PerCaM-Health improves graph recovery, dynamic edge tracking, and intervention direction accuracy compared to cohort-level, per-patient, and non-personalized temporal baselines. These results demonstrate that jointly modeling personalization and temporal evolution yields more reliable causal structure and intervention reasoning.

</details>


### [179] [Bifurcation Models: Learning Set-Valued Solution Maps with Weight-Tied Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07277)
*Caleb Jore,Jialin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分岔模型（bifurcation models），通过权重共享的动力系统视角，让不同初始化收敛到不同稳定平衡点，从而学习解集的吸引子景观，无需人工标注分支标签即可发现多个有效解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学和组合问题存在多个正确解，但标准监督学习只选择一个作为标签，这个隐式选择器可能是任意、不连续的，比解集本身更难学习。

Method: 提出分岔模型，采用权重共享的动力系统观点，不同初始化收敛到不同稳定平衡点，模型表示吸引子景观而非单个分支。理论上证明宽泛的集值映射（具有局部Lipschitz分支）可由正则平衡动力学表示，且诱导选择器几乎处处正则。

Result: 在 frustrated Ising 模型上，该动力学无需分支标签即可发现多个有效平衡，且优于单分支监督方法。Allen-Cahn 实验表明多样性不会自动出现，需要显式鼓励，但存在准确率-多样性的权衡。

Conclusion: 分岔模型为学习多解问题提供了一种无需人工标注分支标签的有效框架，但需注意多样性需显式激励，且存在与准确率的权衡。

Abstract: Many scientific and combinatorial problems admit multiple correct solutions, not a single label. Standard supervised learning resolves this ambiguity by choosing one solution as the target, but this hidden selector can be arbitrary, discontinuous, and harder to learn than the underlying solution set. We study bifurcation models, a weight-tied dynamical view in which different initializations can converge to different stable equilibria, so the model represents an attractor landscape rather than one chosen branch. We prove that broad set-valued maps with locally Lipschitz branches can be represented by regular equilibrium dynamics and that the induced selectors are almost everywhere regular, while manual selectors can be arbitrarily irregular. Experiments on frustrated Ising models show that such dynamics can discover multiple valid equilibria without branch labels and outperform single-branch supervision. Allen--Cahn experiments further show that diversity is not automatic: it can be encouraged explicitly, but with an accuracy--diversity tradeoff.

</details>


### [180] [Predictive but Not Plannable: RC-aux for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07278)
*Wenyuan Li,Guang Li,Keisuke Maeda,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RC-aux辅助目标，通过时间轴上的多步预测和空间轴上的预算条件可达性监督，解决潜世界模型中短时预测准确但长程规划空间错位的问题，在LeWorldModel上实现更优的规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型常以局部预测训练，但被用于长程目标导向搜索，导致潜空间中欧氏距离无法反映有限动作预算下的实际可达性，出现时空错位问题。

Method: RC-aux保持世界模型主干不变，沿时间轴引入多步开放环预测，沿空间轴引入预算条件可达性监督与时序硬负样本，并设计可达性感知规划器。

Result: 在目标条件像素控制任务和LIBERO-Goal扩展上，RC-aux以较小额外代价显著提升了LeWM风格的规划性能。

Conclusion: 潜世界模型的规划不仅依赖预测精度，还依赖表示是否编码了下游搜索所需的时间与几何结构。

Abstract: A latent world model may achieve accurate short-horizon prediction while still inducing a latent space that is poorly aligned with planning. A key issue is spatiotemporal mismatch: these models are often trained with local predictive supervision, but deployed for long-horizon goal-directed search in latent spaces where Euclidean distance may not reflect what is reachable within a finite action budget. We present the Reachability-Correction auxiliary objective (RC-aux), a lightweight correction for this mismatch in reconstruction-free latent world models. RC-aux keeps the world-model backbone unchanged and adds planning-aligned supervision along two axes. Along the time axis, multi-horizon open-loop prediction trains the model beyond one-step consistency. Along the space axis, budget-conditioned reachability supervision, together with temporal hard negatives, encourages the latent space to distinguish states that are eventually reachable from those reachable within the current planning horizon. At test time, the learned reachability signal can also be used by a reachability-aware planner to favor trajectories that are both goal-directed and attainable under the available budget. We instantiate RC-aux on LeWorldModel and evaluate it under both continuation-training and matched-from-scratch settings. Across goal-conditioned pixel-control tasks and a LIBERO-Goal extension, RC-aux improves LeWM-style planning with modest additional cost. These results suggest that planning with latent world models depends not only on predictive accuracy, but also on whether the learned representation encodes the temporal and geometric structure required by downstream search. The code is available at https://github.com/Guang000/RC-aux.

</details>


### [181] [Mask2Cause: Causal Discovery via Adjacency Constrained Causal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07280)
*Omar Muhammad,Pasupuleti Dhruv Shivkant,Deepak N. Subramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mask2Cause是一个端到端框架，通过创新性的倒置变量嵌入和邻域约束掩码注意力机制，在前向传播过程中直接恢复因果图，在多个基准测试上实现了最先进的因果发现性能，并可将预测模型参数减少70%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经方法存在两个主要问题：一是基于组件架构无法捕捉共享系统动态，二是采用解耦的后处理图提取方法容易过拟合伪相关。

Method: 提出Mask2Cause端到端框架，包含倒置变量嵌入和邻域约束掩码注意力机制，通过同方差或异方差目标函数进行训练，在预测前向传播过程中直接恢复因果图。

Result: 在从合成混沌动力学到真实生物模拟等多种基准上实现了最先进的因果发现，显著降低了参数复杂度，并能将预测模型参数平均减少70%以上同时保持预测准确性。

Conclusion: Mask2Cause通过端到端学习方式有效解决了现有方法的局限性，在因果发现效率和模型压缩方面均展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Leveraging deep learning for causal discovery in time series remains challenging because existing neural methods predominantly rely on component-wise architectures that fail to capture shared system dynamics or employ decoupled post-hoc graph extraction that risks overfitting to spurious correlations. We propose $\textbf{Mask2Cause}$, an end-to-end framework that recovers the underlying causal graph directly during the forecasting forward pass. Our approach introduces an Inverted Variable Embedding and an Adjacency-Constrained Masked Attention mechanism, trained with homoscedastic or heteroscedastic objectives to capture causal influences in both mean and variance. Empirical results on diverse benchmarks, from synthetic chaotic dynamics to realistic biological simulations, demonstrate state-of-the-art causal discovery with significantly reduced parameter complexity compared to standard baselines. We further show that inferred causal structures can be used to reduce parameter count of forecasting models by more than 70% on average while maintaining predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [182] [The Convergence Gap: Instruction-Tuned Language Models Stabilize Later in the Forward Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07282)
*Yifan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现指令微调后的检查点比预训练检查点更晚收敛到最终预测分布，并将这种“收敛差距”归因于晚期MLP层的计算延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解指令微调（instruction tuning）如何改变模型内部预测行为的动态过程，揭示“收敛差距”（convergence gap）这一诊断指标。

Method: 设计模型差分诊断方法，逐层解码每个transformer层的下一个词预测分布，测量其与模型最终输出分布的KL散度；通过匹配前缀干预、端点读出、历史回放等实验，以及晚期MLP层移植实验进行因果验证。

Result: 指令微调检查点在更深的层中仍远离最终预测；晚期MLP层移植实验表明，将指令微调晚期MLP移植到预训练模型中会增加KL差距（+0.34 nats），反向操作则减少差距（-0.51 nats），远强于随机扰动。

Conclusion: 指令微调后的模型倾向于“更晚收敛”，晚期MLP层是控制这种预测延迟的最强双向因果手柄。

Abstract: Final outputs hide when a checkpoint commits to its next-token prediction. We introduce the convergence gap, a model-diffing diagnostic that decodes each layer's next-token distribution and measures its distance to the model's own final distribution. Across six paired pretrained and instruction-tuned checkpoints in native prompting regimes, instruction-tuned checkpoints remain farther from their final predictions later into the stack. The effect persists under endpoint-matched raw and tuned readouts, endpoint-free same-history checks, and fixed-history template replay. Matched-prefix interventions identify late MLP windows as the largest tested leverage point: late IT grafts into PT hosts increase late KL by +0.34 nats, while PT-late swaps into IT hosts reduce it by -0.51 nats; matched random late perturbations give only +0.003 versus +0.327 for the true late graft. A preselected Gemma case study provides behavior-facing plausibility for the same late swap, without serving as a benchmark claim. These results identify a robust predictiondynamics signature of post-training: released instruction-following checkpoints tend to settle later, and late MLP computation is the strongest tested bidirectional handle on that delay under matched histories.

</details>


### [183] [Instruction Tuning Changes How Upstream State Conditions Late Readout: A Cross-Patching Diagnostic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07284)
*Yifan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过“首次分歧交叉修补”方法，研究了指令微调模型在早期层与后期层之间的协同机制，发现后期层的效果依赖于其自身的上游状态，而非仅来自基础模型的早期层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作发现模型内部存在控制行为（如拒绝方向、助手轴等）的句柄，但这些结果多集中在中间或后期层。本文想进一步探究：早期计算与后期层如何协同工作，将内部状态差异转化为最终的 token 输出差异。

Method: 提出“首次分歧交叉修补”方法：在预训练基座（PT）与指令微调（IT）模型首次产生输出分歧的 token 位置，交叉替换各模型的早期层状态与后期层堆栈，从而分离不同训练策略对模型行为的影响。

Result: IT后期层从IT上游状态获得+2.44 logits增益，从PT上游仅获+0.76；交互效应+1.68，在所有5个密集模型家族（4B-32B）中均为正。最终MLP层中的稀疏特征部分中介了该效应，且受上游状态驱动。

Conclusion: 后期层的效果并非自包含的，其发挥更大作用的前提是读取自身经指令微调的上游状态。对定位于后期层的成对检查点研究，应在替换上游状态后验证效果是否仍然存在。

Abstract: Recent interpretability work has identified model-internal handles on post-trained behavior, including refusal directions, assistant/persona axes, and sparse chat-tuning features. These results localize where behaviors can be read out or controlled, often in middle-to-late layers. We ask how earlier computation and the late stack cooperate to turn those differences into next-token margins. To test this, we introduce first-divergence cross-patching: at the first token where pretrained base (PT) and instruction-tuned (IT) checkpoints disagree, we cross each model's earlier-layer state with each model's late stack. The diagnostic separates training recipes: same-base instruction-following descendants show late effects that depend on their own earlier-layer state, while OpenMath2 math-domain SFT and controlled code/biomed CPT controls with verified domain learning do not; for OpenMath2, the late effect is already largely portable from base earlier-layer state. Across five dense families (4B-32B), the IT late stack adds +0.76 logits from PT upstream and +2.44 from IT upstream, giving a +1.68 interaction that is positive in every family. Thus the late stack has a real PT-upstream effect, but its larger effect in the IT checkpoint appears only when it reads its own post-trained upstream state. Sparse features in final MLP layers partially mediate the effect and are driven by upstream patches, supporting a handoff from earlier state to final-layer feature activation to IT-token margin. Forced-token scoring shows that the local token choice can change later exact-answer success. Operationally, paired-checkpoint studies that localize a difference to late layers should test whether it survives under the other checkpoint's upstream state before treating the late stack as self-contained.

</details>


### [184] [Pretraining Induces a Reusable Spectral Basis for Downstream Task Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07302)
*Junjie Yu,Yue Wang,Zihan Deng,Yan Zhu,Wenxiao Ma,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现预训练模型微调时，参数空间中存在一组高度稳定的方向（即预训练权重矩阵的主奇异向量），这些方向在不同下游任务中保持稳定且共享，并非无关噪声，而是编码了可迁移的结构信息。基于此提出冻结奇异向量、仅优化谱系数的参数高效微调方法，在GLUE上仅用0.2%可训练参数取得竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注微调发生在低维子空间这一现象，却忽视了未被探索的方向为何稳定——这些方向是无关噪声还是已编码任务相关结构？理解这一问题对揭示预训练知识如何迁移至关重要。

Method: 对视觉和语言模型进行系统谱分析，观察预训练权重矩阵主奇异向量在微调前后的变化，并跨不同下游任务进行对比。提出参数高效微调方法：冻结预训练奇异向量，仅优化对应的谱系数。

Result: 预训练权重矩阵的主奇异向量在微调中高度稳定，且在不同无关下游任务间共享；在更大数据集上预训练的模型表现出更高的谱稳定性；提出的方法在GLUE上仅用0.2%可训练参数达到竞争性能。

Conclusion: 预训练发现的稳定方向编码了可迁移的结构信息而非噪声，成功的预训练建立了下游任务继承并运行的谱坐标系，预训练规模与几何可迁移性直接相关。

Abstract: Finetuning pretrained models occurs in a low-dimensional subspace of the full parameter space. Prior work has focused on characterizing this optimization subspace, but largely ignored the complementary question: why do certain directions remain unexplored during finetuning? Are these stable directions irrelevant to downstream tasks, or do they already encode task-relevant structure that requires no further adjustment? Answering this question is central to understanding how pretrained knowledge transfers. Through systematic spectral analysis across vision and language models, we show that the leading singular vectors of pretrained weight matrices remain highly stable under finetuning and are shared across unrelated downstream tasks, revealing that pretraining establishes a reusable spectral coordinate system. Models pretrained on larger datasets exhibit greater spectral stability under distribution shift or task change, directly linking pretraining scale to geometric transferability. Motivated by these findings, we propose a parameter-efficient method that freezes pretrained singular vectors and optimizes only leading spectral coefficients, achieving competitive performance on GLUE with 0.2% trainable parameters. Our results reveal that the stable directions encode transferable structure rather than irrelevant noise: successful pretraining discovers spectral bases that downstream tasks inherit and operate within.

</details>


### [185] [Latent Order Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07304)
*Emil Carlsson,Newton Mwai,Fredrik D. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出潜序老虎机（LOB）模型，仅需动作偏好的偏序关系作为先验知识，在放松传统潜老虎机假设的同时仍能有效利用跨实例结构，并给出了UCB和贝叶斯采样两种算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统潜老虎机算法需要精确已知的潜状态和后验分布，且少量状态难以刻画所有实例的奖励分布；实际中同一状态的实例可能共享偏好顺序但奖励尺度不同，现有方法无法处理这种变异。

Method: 提出潜序老虎机（LOB），仅假设每种潜状态下动作的偏序关系已知，允许同一状态的实例有不同奖励分布；设计基于UCB的算法和基于后验采样的算法，分别适用于全序和偏序潜状态。

Result: 给出了UCB算法的遗憾上界；实验表明，当同状态实例共享奖励参数时，LOB与全先验潜老虎机相当，当奖励尺度不同时LOB更优。

Conclusion: LOB在放松先验假设的同时保持了竞争力，为实际中只能获得部分偏好信息的个性化决策问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Bandit algorithms solve diverse sequential decision-making problems, but are often too sample-inefficient for from-scratch personalization. To substantially reduce exploration times, latent bandit algorithms exploit cross-instance structure implied by discrete latent states, provided that the posterior distribution of rewards and latent states is known and accurate. However, obtaining an accurate model of this structure is difficult, and a small number of latent states may be insufficient to characterize the reward distributions in all problem instances. We propose latent order bandits (LOB), relaxing the assumptions of latent bandits to require only prior knowledge of a partial order of action preferences in each state. This allows instances of the same state to vary in reward distributions, as long as the partial order of actions is shared. For example, groups of users on a streaming service may agree on which movie genres are the best but rate experiences on different scales. We give an upper-confidence bound procedure for the LOB problem, applicable to both total and partial latent orders, and give an upper bound on its regret. To improve empirical performance, we propose a posterior-sampling algorithm and show, in a suite of experiments, that both are competitive with full-prior latent bandits when same-state instances share reward parameters, and preferable to them when reward scales differ between instances with the same latent state.

</details>


### [186] [Generative Modeling with Flux Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07319)
*Peter Pao-Huang,Xiaojie Qiu,Stefano Ermon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flux Matching 是一种新的生成模型范式，通过放宽对向量场的约束条件（不要求其为保守场），使得模型可以学习到无数个以数据为平稳分布的向量场，从而在保持强性能的同时实现了更快的采样、可解释的动力学和定向依赖编码等新功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于分数匹配的生成模型要求向量场必须是保守场（即等于数据得分），这种强约束限制了模型的设计灵活性和应用范围。

Method: 提出 Flux Matching 目标函数，仅要求向量场的平稳分布为数据分布，而不要求向量场等于数据得分，从而允许学习无数个合法向量场。

Result: 在高维图像数据集上表现强劲；实现了更快的采样速度、可解释的机械论模型、以及编码变量间定向依赖的动力学。

Conclusion: Flux Matching 将向量场本身从固定目标转变为设计选择，为生成模型开辟了新的维度。

Abstract: We introduce Flux Matching, a new paradigm for generative modeling that generalizes existing score-based models to a broader family of vector fields that need not be conservative. Rather than requiring the model to equal the data score, the Flux Matching objective imposes a weaker condition that admits infinitely many vector fields whose stationary distribution is the data. This flexibility enables a class of generative models that cannot be learned under score matching, in which inductive biases, structural priors, and properties of the dynamics can be directly imposed or optimized. We show that Flux Matching performs strongly on high-dimensional image datasets and, more importantly, that our added freedom unlocks a range of applications including faster sampling, interpretable and mechanistic models, and dynamics that encode directed dependencies between variables. More broadly, Flux Matching opens a new dimension in generative modeling by turning the vector field itself into a design choice rather than a fixed target. Code is available at https://github.com/peterpaohuang/flux_matching.

</details>


### [187] [SparseRL-Sync: Lossless Weight Synchronization with ~100x Less Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07330)
*Lucas Hu,Ranchi Zhao,Isaac Zhu,Zach Zhang,Hscos Zhang,Hugh Yin,Jason Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SparseRL-Sync，利用大模型强化学习训练中参数更新的高度稀疏性（通常>99%），将全权重同步替换为无损稀疏更新（索引+数值），在带宽受限场景下实现约100倍通信量压缩，显著提升吞吐量和端到端效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模强化学习系统中，Trainer-Rollout解耦架构需要频繁同步策略权重；随着模型规模增长，跨数据中心、异构集群等带宽受限场景下，权重同步成为吞吐量和尾延迟的主要瓶颈。

Method: 观察到主流大模型RL训练中参数变更位置具有高度元素级稀疏性（通常99%+），提出SparseRL-Sync：将全权重传输替换为无损稀疏更新载荷（索引+数值），推理端可精确重建，保持100%保真度。结合分桶机制减少启动和控制面开销。

Result: 在简化成本模型下，稀疏同步将每次更新的通信量从S降至约S/X；99%稀疏度（X≈100）下，传输数据量减少约100倍。

Conclusion: SparseRL-Sync显著提升了带宽受限和高异步RL场景下的可扩展性与端到端效率。

Abstract: In large-scale reinforcement learning (RL) systems with decoupled Trainer-Rollout execution, the Trainer must regularly synchronize policy weights to the Rollout side to limit policy staleness. When inter-node bandwidth is abundant, such synchronization is usually only a small fraction of end-to-end cost. As model size grows, however, the communication demand rises rapidly. In bandwidth-constrained or network-variable deployments -- for example, cross-datacenter or cross-cluster settings, heterogeneous resource pools, and online RL -- weight synchronization can become a dominant bottleneck for throughput and tail latency. We observe that, in mainstream large-model RL training, the locations where parameters actually change are highly sparse at the element level (often 99%+ sparsity). Building on this observation, we propose and implement SparseRL-Sync, which replaces full-weight transfers with a lossless sparse update payload (indices and values) that can be exactly reconstructed on the inference side, thereby preserving 100% fidelity. Under a simplified cost model, sparse synchronization reduces the per-update communication volume from S to approximately S/X; with 99% sparsity (X ~ 100), this yields about a 100x reduction in transmitted data. Combined with appropriate bucketing, SparseRL-Sync also reduces launch and control-plane overhead, significantly improving scalability and end-to-end efficiency in bandwidth-limited and highly asynchronous RL settings.

</details>


### [188] [Rethinking Importance Sampling in LLM Policy Optimization: A Cumulative Token Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07331)
*Yuheng Zhang,Chenlu Ye,Shuowei Jin,Changlong Yu,Wei Xiong,Saurabh Sahu,Nan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CTPO方法，通过累积令牌IS比率解决PPO/GRPO（有偏）与全序列比率（高方差）之间的偏差-方差困境，结合位置自适应裁剪，在数学推理任务上取得最优平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法面临偏差-方差困境：PPO/GRPO的令牌级IS比率忽略前缀状态分布差异引入偏差；全序列比率精确但因乘积累积导致高方差；GSPO的长度归一化偏离精确IS校正。需要一种既无偏又低方差的IS比率方法。

Method: 提出累积令牌IS比率（每个位置t的per-token比率乘积），证明其在令牌级策略梯度下提供无偏前缀校正且方差严格低于全序列比率。在此基础上提出CTPO，结合累积令牌IS比率与位置自适应裁剪（根据log空间累计比率的√t增长调整裁剪边界）。

Result: 在工具集成推理场景的多个数学推理基准上，CTPO在两个模型规模下与GRPO和GSPO强基线相比均取得最佳平均性能。

Conclusion: 累积令牌IS比率是解决策略梯度估计中偏差-方差困境的理论上合理方案，CTPO结合该比率与位置自适应裁剪能够有效提升RLVR在数学推理任务上的表现。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning, including reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), has emerged as a powerful approach for LLM post-training. Central to these approaches is the design of the importance sampling (IS) ratio used in off-policy policy-gradient estimation. Existing methods face a fundamental bias-variance dilemma: token-level IS ratios, as adopted by PPO (Schulman et al., 2017) and GRPO (Shao et al., 2024), introduce bias by ignoring prefix state distribution mismatch; full sequence ratios provide exact trajectory-level correction but suffer from high variance due to the multiplicative accumulation of per-token ratios, while GSPO (Zheng et al., 2025) improves numerical stability via length normalization at the cost of deviating from the exact full-sequence IS correction. In this work, we identify the cumulative token IS ratio, the product of per-token ratios up to position $t$, as a theoretically principled solution to this dilemma. We prove that, under the token-level policy-gradient formulation, this ratio provides an unbiased prefix correction for each token-level gradient term and has strictly lower variance than the full sequence ratio. Building on this insight, we propose CTPO (Cumulative Token Policy Optimization), which combines the cumulative token IS ratio with position-adaptive clipping that scales log-space clip bounds according to the natural $\sqrt{t}$ growth of the cumulative log-ratio. This yields more consistent regularization across token positions. We implement and evaluate CTPO in the tool-integrated reasoning setting on several challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks, achieving the best average performance across both model scales compared with strong GRPO and GSPO baselines. Code will be available at https://github.com/horizon-llm/CTPO.

</details>


### [189] [Beyond Linear Attention: Softmax Transformers Implement In-Context Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07333)
*Zixuan Xie,Xinyu Liu,Claire Chen,Shuze Daniel Liu,Rohan Chandra,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从理论上分析了使用标准softmax注意力的Transformer在上下文强化学习（ICRL）中的工作机制，证明其前向传播等价于加权softmax时序差分（TD）学习算法的迭代更新，并证明了在特定参数下策略评估误差随层数增加而衰减，且这些参数是预训练损失的全局最小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ICRL理论分析大多依赖线性注意力假设（将softmax替换为单位映射），这与实际使用的softmax注意力不符。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为实际ICRL应用提供理论基础。

Method: 论文证明了具有softmax注意力的Transformer的前向传播等价于加权softmax TD学习算法的迭代更新；在核空间中建立了该算法的策略评估框架；通过收缩条件分析了误差衰减特性；并证明了相关参数是预训练损失的全局极小值。

Result: 1）softmax注意力Transformer的前向传播等价于加权softmax TD学习迭代；2）加权softmax TD统一了线性TD和表格型TD作为特例；3）在特定收缩条件下，策略评估误差随层数增加而衰减；4）所识别的参数是预训练损失的全局最小值。

Conclusion: 本文为实际使用的softmax注意力Transformer在ICRL中的工作机制提供了严格的理论理解，揭示了其与加权softmax TD学习算法的等价关系，为理解Transformer的上下文学习能力提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: In-context reinforcement learning (ICRL) studies agents that, after pretraining, adapt to new tasks by conditioning on additional context without parameter updates. Existing theoretical analyses of ICRL largely rely on linear attention, which replaces the softmax function in the standard attention with an identity mapping. This paper provides the first theoretical understanding of ICRL without making the unrealistic linear attention simplification. In particular, we consider the standard softmax attention used in practice. We show that, with certain parameters, the layerwise forward pass of a Transformer with such softmax attention is equivalent to iterative updates of a weighted softmax temporal difference (TD) learning algorithm. Here, weighted softmax TD is a new RL algorithm that performs policy evaluation in kernel space and adopts both linear TD and tabular TD as special cases. We also prove that under a certain contraction condition, the policy evaluation error decays as the number of layers grows, with the identified parameters above.
