Finally, we prove that those parameters are a global minimizer of a pretraining loss, explaining their emergence in our numerical experiments.

</details>


### [190] [CellScientist: Dual-Space Hierarchical Orchestration for Closed-Loop Refinement of Virtual Cell Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07335)
*Mengran Li,Bo Li,Jiaying Wang,Wenbin Xing,Yixuan Dong,Chengyang Zhang,Hongliang Zhang,Yuzhong Peng,Jinlin Wu,Bob Zhang,Bingo Wing-Kuen Ling,Fuji Yang,Zhen Lei,Jiebo Luo,Zelin Zang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CellScientist提出一种双空间层次框架，将高层假设空间与低层可执行实现空间耦合，解决大语言模型辅助虚拟细胞建模中的“精炼-路由”问题，使执行失败成为结构化信号而非调试事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM辅助建模工作流存在精炼-路由问题：预测偏差通过可执行实现被发现，但相关修正可能涉及建模假设、表征设计、实现或任务约束等多个层次，缺乏结构化反馈传播导致迭代精炼可能只修复代码而忽略根本假设错误。

Method: 提出CellScientist双空间层次框架：(1) 高层假设空间表示建模决策为结构化状态；(2) 低层可执行实现空间将假设转化为符合任务和接口约束的可接受程序；(3) 执行偏差被路由回目标假设或实现更新，形成闭环 Hypothesis -> Implementation -> Hypothesis。

Result: 在形态学、转录组学基准测试及单细胞扰动评估中，CellScientist选出的最终可执行模型在固定划分和评估协议下优于参考基线，且工作流产生可审计的精炼轨迹。

Conclusion: CellScientist通过结构化反馈传播闭环，将模型失效从调试事件转变为模型精炼的结构化信号，实现了更可靠的可审计建模过程。

Abstract: Virtual Cell Modeling (VCM) requires models that not only predict perturbation responses, but also support targeted revision when predictions fail. Current LLM-assisted modeling workflows face a refinement-routing problem: prediction discrepancies are observed through executable implementations, but the relevant revision may involve the modeling assumption, representation design, implementation, or task constraint. Without structured feedback propagation across these levels, iterative refinement may repair code while failing to revise the assumption responsible for the discrepancy. We propose CellScientist, a dual-space hierarchical framework that couples a high-level hypothesis space with a low-level executable implementation space. CellScientist represents modeling decisions as structured states, realizes them as admissible programs under task and interface constraints, and routes execution discrepancies back to targeted hypothesis or implementation updates. This enables a closed Hypothesis -> Implementation -> Hypothesis loop where failures become structured signals for model refinement rather than debugging events. Across morphology and transcriptomic benchmarks, with additional single-cell perturbation evaluations, the final executable models selected by CellScientist improve over reference baselines under fixed split and evaluation protocols, while the workflow produces auditable refinement traces.

</details>


### [191] [Mage: Multi-Axis Evaluation of LLM-Generated Executable Game Scenes Beyond Compile-Pass Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07342)
*Hugh Xuechen Liu,Kıvanç Tatar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现编译通过率在多组件领域特定工件生成中具有误导性，并提出四轴评估框架Mage，实验证明编译率与功能正确性呈负相关，多维度评估才能有效检测生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成主要依赖编译通过率作为评估信号，但该指标在多组件领域特定工件（如游戏场景）中可能产生误导，亟需更全面的评估体系。

Method: 提出Mage四轴评估协议：编译成功、运行时成功、结构保真度、机制遵循度。在858次生成尝试中，对4个开源LLM（7B-30B）和26个Unity目标模式可玩概念进行实验，对比直接NL-to-C#生成与结构IR条件生成两种方式。

Result: 直接NL-to-C#生成运行时通过率最高（平均43%），但生成的结构空洞（机制F1≈0.12）；结构IR条件生成虽降低运行时通过率，但恢复领域忠实结构（F1最高达1.00）；行为级与全场景粒度在统计上无显著差异。

Conclusion: 编译通过率与该领域的功能正确性呈负相关，多轴评估才能有效检测这种分歧，现有单指标评估范式需要重构。

Abstract: Compile-pass rate is the dominant evaluation signal for LLM code generation, yet for multi-component domain-specific artifacts it can be actively misleading. We demonstrate this on executable game scene synthesis with a four-axis evaluation protocol (named `Mage') -- compile success, runtime success, structural fidelity, and mechanism adherence -- applied to 858 generation attempts across four open-weight LLMs (7B--30B), 26~hand-crafted Unity goal pattern playable concepts, and two automatically extracted IR granularity levels. Direct NL-to-C\# generation achieves the highest runtime-pass rate (43\% mean) yet produces structurally vacuous scenes (mechanism $F_1 \approx 0.12$). Structural IR conditioning halves the runtime rate but recovers domain-faithful structure ($F_1$ up to 1.00). Within IR conditioning, behavior-only and full-scene granularity are statistically indistinguishable (McNemar $p = 1.0$), indicating input-level granularity saturation. These results show that compile rate is anti-correlated with functional correctness in this domain and that multi-axis evaluation is necessary to detect the divergence. We release the benchmark, replay logs, and per-record metrics for independent verification.

</details>


### [192] [MISA: Mixture of Indexer Sparse Attention for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07363)
*Ruijie Zhou,Fanxu Meng,Yufei Xu,Tongxuan Liu,Guangming Lu,Muhan Zhang,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MISA方法，通过混合专家路由机制替代DSA索引器的多头评分，在保持精度前提下大幅降低计算开销和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSA索引器使用大量查询头（如64头）对所有前缀token评分，在长上下文场景下成为主要计算瓶颈。

Method: 将DSA的索引器头视为MoE池，通过轻量级路由器基于块级统计信息选择少量活跃头进行细粒度评分，并引入层次化变体通过重排序恢复原始选择。

Result: 仅用8个活跃头即可匹配DSA索引器在LongBench上的性能，在DeepSeek-V3.2和GLM-5上分别减少8倍和4倍索引器头数，TileLang kernel在H200上实现约3.82倍加速。

Conclusion: MISA在不需额外训练的情况下，以极低成本实现了与DSA索引器相当的精度和更好的效率，为稀疏注意力推理提供了实用优化方案。

Abstract: DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA) sets the state of the art for fine-grained inference-time sparse attention by introducing a learned token-wise indexer that scores every prefix token and selects the most relevant ones for the main attention. To remain expressive, the indexer uses many query heads (for example, 64 on DeepSeek-V3.2) that share the same selected token set; this multi-head design is precisely what makes the indexer the dominant cost on long contexts. We propose MISA (Mixture of Indexer Sparse Attention), a drop-in replacement for the DSA indexer that treats its indexer heads as a pool of mixture-of-experts. A lightweight router uses cheap block-level statistics to pick a query-dependent subset of only a few active heads, and only those heads run the heavy token-level scoring. This preserves the diversity of the original indexer pool while reducing the per-query cost from scoring every prefix token with every head to scoring it with only a handful of routed heads, plus a negligible router term computed on a small set of pooled keys. We further introduce a hierarchical variant of MISA that uses the routed pass to keep an enlarged candidate set and then re-ranks it with the original DSA indexer to recover the final selected tokens almost exactly. With only eight active heads and no additional training, MISA matches the dense DSA indexer on LongBench across DeepSeek-V3.2 and GLM-5 while running with eight and four times fewer indexer heads respectively, and outperforms HISA on average. It also preserves fully green Needle-in-a-Haystack heatmaps up to a 128K-token context and recovers more than 92% of the tokens selected by the DSA indexer per layer. Our TileLang kernel delivers roughly a 3.82 times speedup over DSA's original indexer kernel on a single NVIDIA H200 GPU.

</details>


### [193] [StreamPhy: Streaming Inference of High-Dimensional Physical Dynamics via State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07384)
*Panqi Chen,Yifan Sun,Shikai Fang,Xiao Fu,Lei Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StreamPhy是一个端到端框架，能够从不规则稀疏测量数据中高效实时推断高维物理场的完整动力学，在精度和推理速度上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如扩散生成模型和函数张量方法）通常在离线设置下运行，依赖完整时间观测或推理成本高昂，无法满足实时、不规则稀疏测量的在线推断需求。

Method: StreamPhy包含三个核心组件：数据自适应观测编码器（鲁棒于任意观测模式）、结构化状态空间模型（支持跨不规则时间间隔的在线更新）、以及函数张量特征线性调制（FT-FiLM）解码器用于连续场生成。理论上证明了FT-FiLM比函数Tucker模型更具表达力。

Result: 在三个代表性物理系统上，StreamPhy在所有挑战性采样模式下一致优于基线方法，精度提升至少48%，推理速度比扩散方法快20-100倍。

Conclusion: StreamPhy为高维多模态物理场的实时流式推断提供了一种高效且准确的解决方案，理论分析和实验均验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Inferring the evolution of high-dimensional and multi-modal (e.g., spatio-temporal) physical fields from irregular sparse measurements in real time is a fundamental challenge in science and engineering. Existing approaches, including diffusion-based generative models and functional tensor methods, typically operate in offline settings, depend on full temporal observations, or incur substantial inference cost. We propose StreamPhy, an end-to-end framework that enables efficient and accurate streaming inference of full-field physical dynamics from incoming irregular sparse measurements. The framework integrates a data-adaptive observation encoder that is robust to arbitrary observation patterns, a structured state-space model that supports memory-efficient online updates across irregular time intervals, and an expressive Functional Tensor Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FT-FiLM) decoder for continuous-field generation. We prove that FT-FiLM is more expressive than the functional Tucker model, admitting a richer function class for handling complex dynamics. Experiments on three representative physical systems under challenging sampling patterns show that StreamPhy consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, with at least 48\% improvement in accuracy and up to 20--100X faster inference than diffusion-based methods.

</details>


### [194] [Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07386)
*Karthick Krishna M.,Haricharan Balasundaram,Rahul Vaze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了嵌套演化可行集上的凸优化问题（CONES），提出了懒惰算法和Frugal算法，分别实现了后悔值与移动成本之间的权衡，并证明了最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将嵌套凸体追踪问题推广到优化视角，研究在线算法如何在保证可行性的同时，最小化后悔值与移动成本。

Method: 针对凸损失函数提出懒惰算法（Lazy-algorithm），针对强凸或α-sharp损失函数提出Frugal算法。

Result: 懒惰算法实现O(T^{1-β})后悔与O(T^β)移动成本的权衡；Frugal算法实现零后悔与O(log T)移动成本，且下界证明该结果最优。

Conclusion: Frugal算法在强凸和α-sharp损失函数下达到最优的零后悔与对数级移动成本，理论下界证明其不可改进。

Abstract: Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets (CONES)} is considered where the objective function $f$ remains fixed but the feasible region evolves over time as a nested sequence $S_1 \supseteq S_2 \supseteq \cdots \supseteq S_T$. The goal of an online algorithm is to simultaneously minimize the regret with respect to hindsight static optimal benchmark and the total movement cost while ensuring feasibility at all times. CONES is an optimization-oriented generalization of the well-known nested convex body chasing problem. When the loss function is convex, we propose a lazy-algorithm and show that it achieves $O(T^{1-β}), O(T^β)$ simultaneous regret and movement cost for any $β\in (0,1]$, over a time horizon of $T$. When the loss function is strongly convex or $α$-sharp, we propose an algorithm Frugal that simultaneously achieves zero regret and a movement cost of $O(\log T)$. To complement this, we show that any online algorithm with $o(T)$ regret has a movement cost of $Ω(\log{T})$ for both cases, proving optimality of Frugal.

</details>


### [195] [Rubric-based On-policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07396)
*Junfeng Fang,Zhepei Hong,Mao Zheng,Mingyang Song,Gengsheng Li,Houcheng Jiang,Dan Zhang,Haiyun Guo,Xiang Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ROPD框架，用结构化语义评分标准（rubrics）替代教师logits，实现仅基于教师生成响应的黑盒在线策略蒸馏（OPD），在多数场景下超越先进logit-based OPD方法，样本效率提升可达10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线策略蒸馏（OPD）依赖教师logits，这限制了其在白盒场景中的应用。作者认为结构化语义评分（structured semantic rubrics）可以作为教师logits的可扩展替代方案，从而仅使用教师生成的响应即可实现OPD。

Method: ROPD从教师-学生对比中生成提示特定的评分标准（rubrics），然后利用这些rubrics对学生生成的rollout进行评分，用于在线策略优化。

Result: ROPD在大多数场景下优于先进的基于logits的OPD方法，样本效率提升高达10倍。

Conclusion: 基于评分标准的OPD可作为灵活、兼容黑盒的替代方案，替代主流的基于logits的OPD，为跨闭源和开源大语言模型的可扩展蒸馏提供了简单而强大的基线。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is a powerful paradigm for model alignment, yet its reliance on teacher logits restricts its application to white-box scenarios. We contend that structured semantic rubrics can serve as a scalable alternative to teacher logits, enabling OPD using only teacher-generated responses. To prove it, we introduce ROPD, a simple yet foundational framework for rubric-based OPD. Specifically, ROPD induces prompt-specific rubrics from teacher-student contrasts, and then utilizes these rubrics to score the student rollouts for on-policy optimization. Empirically, ROPD outperforms the advanced logit-based OPD methods across most scenarios, and achieving up to a 10x gain in sample efficiency. These results position rubric-based OPD as a flexible, black-box-compatible alternative to the prevailing logit-based OPD, offering a simple yet strong baseline for scalable distillation across proprietary and open-source LLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/Peregrine123/ROPD_official.

</details>


### [196] [Have Graph -- Will Lift? The Case for Higher-Order Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07397)
*Bastian Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文讨论了几何和拓扑在机器学习中站稳脚跟的现状，指出虽然模型架构多样化，但缺乏合适的基准数据集，呼吁社区除了对现有图数据集进行高阶信息提升外，还应积极寻找新的数据集来支撑研究领域的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于几何/拓扑深度学习模型日益多样，但合适的基准数据集严重匮乏，研究者常只能对现有图数据集进行高阶信息提升，缺乏真正能支撑领域发展的原始数据集。

Method: 这是一篇观点论文，通过分析当前几何/拓扑深度学习领域的现状（模型多样 vs. 数据集稀缺），提出社区应主动寻找并贡献新的数据集。

Result: 指出当前领域存在模型多样性与基准数据集稀缺性之间的鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 呼吁社区除了对现有图数据集进行高阶信息提升外，更要积极发掘和提供新的数据集，以夯实拓扑深度学习的研究基础。

Abstract: After a somewhat rocky start, geometry and topology have established a foothold in machine learning. Message passing, either on graphs or higher-order complexes, is one of the main drivers of geometric deep learning, and paradigms that were once considered to be firmly in the realm of the abstract-like sheaves-have been "tamed" to serve as novel inductive biases for model architectures in topological deep learning. The veritable diversity of models, however, is in stark contrast to the scarcity of suitable benchmark datasets. As a result, researchers often resort to lifting existing graph datasets to include higher-order information. In this opinion paper, I want to encourage the community to also source new datasets, which may be used to prop up the foundations of our research field.

</details>


### [197] [Emergent Symbolic Structure in Health Foundation Models: Extraction, Alignment, and Cross-Modal Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07407)
*Gajendra Katuwal,Advait Koparkar,Salar Abbaspourazad,Anshuman Mishra,Sarvesh Kirthivasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种后训练框架，将冻结的健康基础模型嵌入分解为可解释的符号方向，并利用这些符号实现跨模态对齐，无需重新训练即可保留95%以上的域内性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 健康基础模型从可穿戴传感器学习有用表征，但解释其编码内容以及在训练后跨模态迁移知识仍然困难。

Method: 提出后训练框架：将冻结的嵌入分解为可解释的符号方向，利用这些符号对齐嵌入空间，无需重新训练。在三种分别预训练于~2000万分钟、~17.2万参与者数据的PPG和加速度计基础模型上评估，并在3万受试者验证集上分析。

Result: 提取的符号选择性地与健康状况和生理属性关联，且这些关联部分跨模态和架构共享。跨模态迁移保留95%以上域内性能，接近对称，在有限配对数据下饱和。

Conclusion: 健康基础模型嵌入包含可解释的符号化组织，该组织跨模态共享，支持无需联合训练的跨域迁移。

Abstract: Health foundation models (FMs) learn useful representations from wearable sensors, but interpreting what they encode and transferring that knowledge across modalities after training remains difficult. We present a post-training framework that decomposes frozen embeddings into interpretable directions, referred to as symbols, and use these symbols to align the embedding spaces without retraining. We evaluate the framework on three FMs for photoplethysmography (PPG) and accelerometer data, independently pretrained on ~20M minutes of unlabeled data from ~172K participants, and analyzed on a held-out cohort of 30K subjects. We find that extracted symbols associate selectively with health conditions and physiological attributes, and these associations are partially shared across modalities and architectures. Cross-modal transfer via symbols retains more than 95% of in-domain performance, is nearly symmetric across domain directions, and saturates with limited paired data, together indicating that alignment recovers a shared low-dimensional subspace rich in physiological information. Overall, these results suggest that health FM embeddings contain an interpretable symbolic organization that is shared across modalities and supports cross-domain transfer without joint training.

</details>


### [198] [Tracking Large-scale Shared Bikes with Inertial Motion Learning in GNSS Blocked Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07412)
*Feng Liu,Kejia Li,Zhiwei Yang,Chunwei Yang,Qun Li,Guobin Wu,Qiang Ni,Ruipeng Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合自行车机械约束与混合专家模型的惯性追踪框架，利用踏板-后轮传动关系将骑行者的周期性踩踏行为转化为车轮速度进行动态校准，在共享单车真实数据上实现了至少12%的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低成本惯性传感器定位存在累积漂移和鲁棒性差的问题，而视觉/LiDAR方案不适合大规模部署，因此需要一种仅依赖惯性传感器的高精度自行车追踪方法。

Method: 提出混合专家模型（MoE），多个专家模块捕获共享表征并通过门控机制加权，实现多任务学习和不确定性感知轨迹估计；基于自行车踏板与后轮机械传动关系，将周期性踩踏行为转化为车轮速度进行动态校准。

Result: 在滴滴出行共享单车真实骑行数据上，系统相比基线方法精度提升至少12%，车轮速度误差在95分位下低于0.5 m/s。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了复杂环境下低成本惯性传感器的定位难题，兼顾了精度与部署可行性。

Abstract: Although Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS) provide a general solution for bike tracking outdoors, there still exist complex riding environments where only inertial navigation systems work, such as urban canyons. Despite decades of research, localization using only low-cost inertial sensors still faces challenges such as cumulative drifts and poor robustness caused by filtering methods. Furthermore, sensors such as visual and LiDAR could provide reliable measurements, but they are not suitable for large-scale deployment. In this paper, we propose an inertial tracking framework that integrates bicycle mechanical constraints with a mixture-of-experts model. Specifically, we leverage multiple expert modules to capture shared representations and weight them through the gating mechanism, thus improving multi-task learning performance and enabling uncertainty-aware trajectory estimation. Furthermore, based on the mechanical transmission between the pedal and the rear wheel of a bike, we explore the intrinsic relationship between the rider's periodic pedalling behaviors and acceleration variations, and convert such patterns into bike's wheel speed for dynamic calibration. Experiments with real-world riding data from shared bikes of the DiDi ride-hailing platform demonstrate that our system improves the accuracy of baselines by at least 12%, with wheel speed errors below 0.5 m/s at 95-percentile.

</details>


### [199] [Risk-Consistent Multiclass Learning from Random Label-Subset Membership Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07413)
*Jiaxu Su,Junpeng Li,Changchun Hua,Yana Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机标签子集查询的弱监督多分类学习框架，通过直接利用查询-响应对（即询问真实标签是否属于某一子集）来训练分类器，并引入风险校正方法解决负经验风险问题，理论和实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统弱监督学习依赖于已有的弱标签对象，但缺乏对直接从标签子集查询响应中生成弱监督信息的系统性研究。实际中获取精确标签成本高或不可靠，而询问标签是否属于某个子集更容易，因此需要建立对应的学习框架。

Method: 建模查询-响应对的数据生成分布，在经验风险最小化（ERM）框架下导出目标风险的无偏估计量；针对负经验风险及过拟合问题，提出基于非负校正和绝对值校正的风险估计器；理论分析包括无偏估计量的条件泛化界与过量风险界，以及校正估计量的偏差与一致性结果。

Result: 在匹配随机查询机制下的实验表明，直接基于查询-响应进行学习是可行的，且风险校正能有效稳定学习过程。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地填补了标签子集查询生成弱监督信息领域的学习框架空白，提出的校正方法在理论和实践上均有效，为实际中更经济的弱监督获取方式提供了理论支撑和方法支持。

Abstract: Obtaining accurate class labels is often costly or unreliable, and may also be limited by privacy or other practical conditions. Compared with asking an annotator to provide the exact class, it is often easier to ask whether the true label belongs to a certain label subset. This query-response form defines a distinct weak-supervision mechanism: weak supervision information is generated through feedback on a label subset. Although weakly supervised learning has studied many learning frameworks, most existing work starts from established weak label objects. A systematic characterization is still lacking for weakly supervised learning generated directly by such query response observations. This paper proposes a multiclass learn ing framework under random label-subset queries. We model the data-generating distribution of query-response observations and derive an unbiased estimator of the target risk under the empirical risk minimization (ERM) framework. To address negative empirical risk and the associated overfitting problem, we introduce corrected risk estimators based on non-negative and absolute-value corrections. Theoretical analysis establishes a conditional generalization and excess-risk bound for the unbiased estimator, and a bias-and-consistency result for the corrected risk estimator. Experiments under the matched random-query mechanism demonstrate the feasibility of direct query-response learning and the stabilization effect of risk correction.

</details>


### [200] [Physical Simulators as Do-Operators: Causal Discovery under Latent Confounders for AI-for-Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07467)
*Tsuyoshi Okita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CFM-SD方法，利用物理模拟器作为do算子，在存在潜在混淆变量且干预成本高昂的条件下进行因果发现，仅需O(d)次单变量干预即可识别d变量因果结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法假设因果充分性（无潜在混淆变量）且依赖合成模拟器中的虚拟干预，但在AI for Science场景（如分子设计、材料科学）中，潜在混淆变量普遍存在，且真实干预（如基于物理的模拟）耗时极长（数小时到数天每数据点），现有方法无法适用。

Method: 提出CFM-SD（Causal Flow Matching with Simulation Data），将第一性原理物理模拟器作为Pearl干预演算中的do算子，同时处理潜在混淆变量和真实干预数据。理论上证明在物理可实现约束下，d变量因果结构只需O(d)次单变量干预即可识别。

Result: 内在评估（合成数据，γ=0.2-0.8）：CFM-SD平均F1=0.800，基线方法F1=0.127-0.562。外在评估（真实科学数据）：分子毒性预测和电池电解质优化中实现57-58%的偏差减少。

Conclusion: CFM-SD在存在潜在混淆变量和高干预成本的现实科学场景中显著优于现有方法，并在实际科学任务中展现了超越合成基准的实用价值。

Abstract: Existing interventional causal discovery methods -- IGSP, DCDI, ENCO -- assume causal sufficiency (no latent confounders) and rely on virtual interventions in synthetic simulators. In AI-for-Science settings such as molecular design and materials science, latent confounders are ubiquitous and real interventions (e.g., physics-based simulations) require hours to days per data point. We propose CFM-SD (Causal Flow Matching with Simulation Data), which uses first-principles physical simulators as do-operators in Pearl's interventional calculus to simultaneously handle latent confounders and real interventional data. Theoretically, $d$-variable causal structure is identifiable with $O(d)$ single-variable interventions -- the minimum under physical realizability constraints. In Intrinsic Evaluation on synthetic data ($γ=0.2$--$0.8$), CFM-SD achieves average F1$=0.800$ vs. F1$=0.127$--$0.562$ for all baselines. In Extrinsic Evaluation on real scientific data, CFM-SD achieves 57--58\% bias reduction in molecular toxicity prediction and battery electrolyte optimization, demonstrating practical value beyond synthetic benchmarks.

</details>


### [201] [SR$^2$-LoRA: Self-Rectifying Inter-layer Relations in Low-Rank Adaptation for Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07420)
*Fengqiang Wan,Yipeng Lin,Kan Lv,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SR²-LoRA方法，通过约束层间关系漂移（即新任务学习中层表征关系的渐进破坏）来缓解参数高效微调中的灾难性遗忘问题。该方法对当前任务样本上新旧模型诱导的关系矩阵进行奇异值对齐，并从理论上证明该对齐方式比逐元素对齐对估计扰动更鲁棒。实验表明该方法有效缓解遗忘，且优势随任务数增加而更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练模型结合参数高效微调在类增量学习中虽有潜力，但适应新任务时仍存在灾难性遗忘问题。现有研究缺乏从层间关系漂移角度对此问题的深入分析。

Method: 提出SR²-LoRA方法，通过构建之前和当前模型在当前任务样本上诱导的关系矩阵，并对其对应的奇异值进行对齐，从而约束层间关系漂移。该方法从理论上证明了奇异值对齐对比逐元素对齐具有更强的鲁棒性。

Result: 在标准CIL基准上，SR²-LoRA有效缓解了灾难性遗忘，并且随着任务数量的增加，方法的优势更加显著。

Conclusion: 层间关系漂移是导致灾难性遗忘的重要因素，通过约束该漂移可以有效缓解遗忘问题。SR²-LoRA是一种简单有效的解决方案，尤其适合任务数量较多的增量学习场景。

Abstract: Pre-trained models with parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) have demonstrated promising potential for class-incremental learning (CIL), yet catastrophic forgetting still persists when adapting models to new tasks. In this paper, we present a novel perspective on catastrophic forgetting through the analysis of inter-layer relation drift, i.e., the progressive disruption of relationships among layer-wise representations during the learning of new tasks. We theoretically show that the increase of such drift reduces the classification margins of previously learned tasks, thereby degrading overall model performance. To address this issue, we propose \underline{S}elf-\underline{R}ectifying inter-layer \underline{R}elation Low-Rank Adaptation~(SR$^2$-LoRA), a simple yet effective method that mitigates catastrophic forgetting by constraining inter-layer relation drift. Specifically, SR$^2$-LoRA constructs the relation matrices induced by the previous and current models on current-task samples, and aligns the corresponding singular values. We further theoretically show that this alignment exhibits greater robustness to estimation perturbations than direct entry-wise alignment. Extensive experiments on standard CIL benchmarks demonstrate that SR$^2$-LoRA effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting, with its advantages becoming more pronounced as the number of tasks increases. Code is available in the \href{https://github.com/FqWan24/SR-2-LoRA}{repository}.

</details>


### [202] [SHRED: Retain-Set-Free Unlearning via Self-Distillation with Logit Demotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07482)
*Zizhao Hu,Ameya Godbole,Johnny Tian-Zheng Wei,Mohammad Rostami,Jesse Thomason,Robin Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SHRED是一种无需保留集的LLM机器遗忘方法，通过识别并仅遗忘高信息量token，同时保留低信息量token作为锚点，实现了遗忘效果与模型效用的帕累托最优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法大多依赖保留集（retain set）来防止模型效用恶化，这增加了数据依赖和部署复杂性。作者希望消除这一依赖，实现无需保留集的遗忘。

Method: 两阶段方法：(1) 选择阶段：对遗忘集实例进行前向传播，收集每个token的自回归概率，选择概率最低（信息量最高）的token作为遗忘位置，其余作为良性锚点。(2) 训练阶段：构建修改后的KL目标，在遗忘位置降低记忆token的logit，在良性位置保持原始分布，通过单一KL自蒸馏目标同时实现遗忘和效用保持。

Result: 在四个标准遗忘基准上，SHRED实现了遗忘效果与模型效用的帕累托最优平衡，超越了依赖保留集的方法。对重新学习攻击和成员推理攻击具有鲁棒性，在多次顺序遗忘后仍能保持稳定的模型效用。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过细粒度token级别的选择性遗忘，可以在不依赖保留集的情况下有效实现LLM遗忘，为实际部署提供了更简洁高效的方案。

Abstract: Machine unlearning for large language models (LLMs) aims to selectively remove memorized content such as private data, copyrighted text, or hazardous knowledge, without costly full retraining. Most existing methods require a retain set of curated examples to prevent catastrophic degradation of general model utility, creating an extra data dependency that complicates deployment. We propose SHRED (Self-distillation via High-surprisal-only Retain-set-free Entropy Demotion), a retain-set-free unlearning method built on a key insight: not all tokens within a forget set instance carry memorized information equally. High-information tokens concentrate the model's memorized knowledge, while low-information tokens reflect general language competence. SHRED operates in two stages. (1) Selection: We perform a forward pass on a forget set instance, collect per-token autoregressive probabilities, and select the bottom (lowest probability, highest Shannon information) as forget positions; the remaining positions are retained as benign anchors. (2) Training: We construct modified KL targets that demote the memorized token's logit at forget positions while preserving the original distribution at benign positions. The model is then trained via a single top KL self-distillation objective that simultaneously drives forgetting and utility preservation. We evaluate SHRED across four standard unlearning benchmarks and demonstrate that it establishes a new Pareto-optimal trade-off between forget efficacy and model utility, outperforming retain-set-dependent methods. Our analysis shows that SHRED is robust against relearning attacks and membership-inference attacks, and it maintains stable utility even after many sequential unlearning runs.

</details>


### [203] [A Flexible Adaptive Stable Clustering Algorithm for Archive-Scale Online Mass Spectrometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07424)
*Shao Shi,Xin Yang,Huiran Feng,Jianhuai Ye,Tianlong Hu,Yaling Zeng,Tzung-May Fu,Lei Zhu,Huizhong Shen,Chen Wang,Shu Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FASC（灵活自适应稳定聚类）框架，通过解耦相似性核与优化逻辑，结合密度增强相似性选择规则和几何约束，实现了可扩展、灵活且稳定的聚类方法。在25万大气气溶胶质谱数据上展现出线性时间复杂度，成功自主绘制了二次无机气溶胶的大气老化路径并分离出超痕量工业示踪物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类方法在可扩展性、度量灵活性和算法稳定性三者之间存在权衡，构成了从大规模质谱数据中提取化学信息的计算瓶颈。

Method: 采用动力学系统框架，架构上解耦相似性核与严格优化逻辑；引入密度增强相似性选择规则和几何约束，保证确定性和顺序无关的收敛。

Result: 在 canonical 机器学习基准上实现>99.5%聚类纯度和0.99调整兰德指数；在2500万个大气气溶胶质谱上呈现严格线性经验运行时间缩放（O(N)），自主识别了二次无机气溶胶老化路径和超稀有工业示踪物。

Conclusion: FASC为挖掘环境大数据提供了一个可扩展的基础设施，有效解决了大规模质谱数据分析中的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Modern online mass spectrometry generates multi-terabyte data streams critical for understanding Earth's environmental systems. However, extracting actionable chemical insights from these repositories is impeded by a computational bottleneck: existing clustering methods force a compromise among scalability, metric flexibility, and algorithmic stability. Here, we introduce Flexible Adaptive Stable Clustering (FASC), a dynamical systems framework that resolves these constraints by architecturally decoupling the similarity kernel from rigorous optimization logic. Unlike legacy heuristics that suffer from stochastic drift and algorithmic blending, FASC employs a Density-Augmented Similarity Selection rule and geometric constraints to guarantee deterministic, order-independent convergence. After validating FASC on canonical machine-learning ground truths (achieving >99.5% cluster purity and 0.99 Adjusted Rand Index), we deployed the framework on 25 million mass spectra of atmospheric aerosols. Demonstrating strictly linear empirical runtime scaling (O(N)), FASC autonomously mapped atmospheric aging pathways of secondary inorganic aerosols while isolating ultra-rare industrial tracers (<0.2% abundance), providing a scalable infrastructure for mining environmental big data.

</details>


### [204] [GameGen-Verifier: Parallel Keypoint-Based Verification for LLM-Generated Games via Runtime State Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07442)
*Chaobo Jia,Ruipeng Wan,Ting Sun,Weihao Tan,Borui Wan,Yuxuan Tong,Guangming Sheng,Hong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GameGen-Verifier自动化验证范式，通过将游戏规范分解为可验证关键点并构建独立验证单元，实现对LLM生成游戏的准确高效验证，在VeriGame数据集上达到92.2%准确率，相比基线方法提升16.6倍速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent-as-a-Verifier方法将验证退化为开放式游戏交互，导致验证结果依赖智能体游戏能力、耗时长、覆盖有限，缺乏可靠的自动化验证手段。

Method: 将规范分解为可验证关键点，并构建独立验证单元；每个单元将游戏运行时修补到具体目标状态，执行有限交互，并根据关键点断言判断结果；实现GGV-Harness可扩展智能体框架，提供并发管理、运行时隔离和故障恢复。

Result: 在包含7个类型100个游戏的VeriGame数据集上，GameGen-Verifier达到92.2%的准确率（人类判断为基准），而基线方法仅58.8%；同时将实际时间减少16.6倍。

Conclusion: GameGen-Verifier通过关键点分解和独立验证单元，有效解决了LLM生成游戏验证中可靠性、覆盖性和效率问题，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: LLM-based game generation promises to turn natural-language specifications into executable games, but progress is limited by the lack of reliable automated verification. Unlike conventional code generation, game correctness is defined over long-horizon interaction: a game may appear correct while violating core mechanics such as state updates, interaction rules, and phase transitions. Existing Agent-as-a-Verifier approaches collapse verification into open-ended gameplay, making verdicts reachability-bound, time-consuming, coverage-limited, and sensitive to the agent's gameplay ability.
  We present GameGen-Verifier, an automated verification paradigm for LLM-generated games that decomposes a specification into verifiable keypoints and grounds them into independent verification units. Each unit patches the game runtime into a concrete target state, executes a bounded interaction, and judges the outcome against the keypoint assertion. We implement GGV-Harness, a scalable agentic harness providing concurrency management, runtime isolation, and fault recovery.
  On VeriGame, our dataset of 100 games across seven genres, GameGen-Verifier achieves up to 92.2% accuracy against human judgments versus 58.8% for the coverage-enforced Agent-as-a-Verifier baseline, while reducing wall-clock time by up to 16.6x.

</details>


### [205] [Excluding the Target Domain Improves Extrapolation: Deconfounded Hierarchical Physics Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07485)
*Tsuyoshi Okita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出去混杂层次门控（DHG）机制，通过反事实估计和后门调整消除温度混杂效应，并结合由粗到细的物理约束，在锂离子电池温度外推任务上实现46%的RMSE提升（0.397→0.215），并发现预训练时排除目标域数据反而提升39%外推性能的反直觉结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理约束深度生成模型在分布外外推时面临两个关键问题：物理定律的层次结构未被充分利用，以及混杂变量（如温度）导致物理不一致性的虚假关联，亟需一种能诊断并消除混杂效应的控制机制。

Method: 提出去混杂层次门控（DHG），包含：(1) 通过do算子进行反事实估计，结合后门调整消除温度混杂；(2) 由粗到细（Coarse-to-Fine）渐进式物理约束；(3) 层次门控动态识别各约束层级受温度混杂污染的程度。

Result: 在锂离子电池温度外推基准（训练24°C，测试4.0-43.0°C）上，DHG达到RMSE=0.215，相比无约束基线（Pure CFM: 0.397）改进46%；预训练时排除目标域数据比包含该数据的外推性能提升39%（RMSE 0.224 vs 0.324）。

Conclusion: DHG有效解决了物理约束生成模型中的混杂问题和层次物理约束缺失问题；FNO在预训练中学到的域无关物理模式在目标域被 withheld 时迁移效果更好，为分布外泛化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Extrapolation to out-of-distribution conditions is a fundamental challenge for physics-constrained deep generative models. Existing methods apply physical constraints as a single static regularization term uniformly across the generation process, and address neither the hierarchical structure of physical laws and the confounding variable problem. We propose the Deconfounded Hierarchical Gate (DHG), which serves as a diagnostic and control mechanism: it identifies when and how strongly temperature confounding contaminates each constraint level, so that hierarchical gates reflect intrinsic physical inconsistency rather than spurious temperature effects. DHG combines counterfactual estimation via the do-operator with backdoor adjustment to remove confounding, then applies Coarse-to-Fine physical constraints progressively. We report a counter-intuitive finding in pretraining: excluding the target-domain data from pretraining outperforms including it by 39% in extrapolation performance (RMSE 0.224 vs. 0.324). This occurs because FNO learns domain-agnostic physical patterns that transfer more effectively when the target domain is withheld. On a lithium-ion battery temperature extrapolation benchmark (trained at 24 degrees Celsius, evaluated at 4.0--43.0 degrees Celsius), our method achieves RMSE = 0.215, a 46% improvement over the unconstrained baseline (Pure CFM: 0.397).

</details>


### [206] [Inference-Time Attribute Distribution Alignment for Unconditional Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07456)
*Hao Luan,See-Kiong Ng,Chun Kai Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对无条件扩散模型在推理时控制生成样本分布（如属性分布对齐）的问题，提出基于最优控制的插拔式方法，无需重新训练即可实现多样化的目标分布对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时可控生成方法主要关注单个样本的控制，难以应用于需要样本群体遵循特定属性分布的场景（如人口统计学平衡或语义比例），因此需要新的方法来解决推理时的属性分布对齐问题。

Method: 将推理时的属性分布对齐形式化为一个最优控制问题：将逆向扩散过程视为动力系统的 rollout，通过添加与时间相关的加性扰动作为控制信号，并利用最优控制算法优化可微的分布匹配目标，同时惩罚控制成本以保持数据保真度。

Result: 在图像生成实验中，所提出的插拔式方法能够在无需重新训练或微调预训练扩散模型的情况下，比基线方法更好地将属性分布对齐到多样化且灵活的测试时目标。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种通用且灵活的控制框架，可在推理阶段高效地调整无条件扩散模型的群体属性分布，具有即插即用的优势。

Abstract: Inference-time controllable generation is essential for real-world applications of unconditional diffusion models. However, most existing techniques focus on individual samples, struggling in applications that require the sample population to follow specific attribute distributions (e.g., demographic balance or semantic proportions). We formalize this setting as the inference-time attribute distributional alignment problem for pretrained unconditional diffusion models. To address this, we cast inference-time attribute distributional alignment as an optimal control problem over the reverse diffusion process, viewing the process as the rollout of a dynamical system and augmenting it with additive, time-dependent perturbations as control. We solve for the perturbations using an optimal-control-based algorithm to optimize a differentiable distribution-matching objective while penalizing control effort to preserve data fidelity. Experiment results in image generation demonstrate that our proposed plug-and-play approach can better align attribute distributions to diverse and flexible test-time targets compared to baselines, without retraining or finetuning the pretrained diffusion model.

</details>


### [207] [Estimation of Motor Unit Parameters from Surface Electromyograms using an Informed Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07458)
*Kaja Balzereit,Malte Mechtenberg,Axel Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息自动编码器的方法，从非侵入式表面肌电信号中同时估计多个运动单元参数（如神经支配区中心、传导速度），在合成数据上取得了良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动单元参数能提升神经力学模型的精度，但现有方法多依赖白盒建模，需要大量人工建模工作，且参数具有个体差异性和随收缩变化的特点，非侵入式估计存在挑战。

Method: 开发了一种信息自动编码器，通过重构表面EMG信号，在潜空间中学习参数并遵循物理规律，将参数与EMG信号相关联，解决带非线性损失函数的逆问题。

Result: 在合成数据上，神经支配区中心估计的平均绝对误差为2.5989 mm，电位的传导速度估计的平均绝对误差为0.1697 m/s。

Conclusion: 该方法能同时估计多个运动单元参数，通过数据驱动的机器学习减少了人工建模工作量，证明了该新方法的可行性。

Abstract: Motor unit parameters such as the innervation zone centre or the conduction velocity of the electrical potential harbour the potential to improve the fidelity of neuromechanical models used for movement and force prediction. Determining these parameters in a non-invasive way is challenging, as they are subject-specific and may vary with muscle contraction. Existing work on the estimation of motor unit parameters mainly relies on white-box modelling and therefore requires substantial manual modelling effort. This work targets the simultaneous estimation of multiple subject-specific motor unit parameters from electromyography (EMG) recordings measured non-invasively at the skin surface. This results in an inverse problem with a nonlinear loss function. To address this problem, an informed autoencoder is developed. This autoencoder reconstructs the surface EMG recordings while learning the parameters in its latent space and adhering to physical laws that relate the parameters to the EMG signals. In experiments on synthetic data, innervation zone centres are estimated with a mean absolute error of 2.5989 $\mathrm{mm}$, and conduction velocities of the electric potential are estimated with a mean absolute error of 0.1697 $\mathrm{m}\mathrm{s}^{-1}$. These results demonstrate the plausibility of this novel approach, which enables the simultaneous estimation of several motor unit parameters while reducing manual modelling effort through the integration of data-driven machine learning.

</details>


### [208] [Ensemble Distributionally Robust Bayesian Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07565)
*Tigran Ramazyan,Denis Derkach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的集成分布鲁棒贝叶斯优化算法，在连续上下文下保持计算可行性，获得了理论上的亚线性遗憾界，改进了现有最优结果，且实验行为与理论保证一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯优化在处理复杂和噪声数据时鲁棒性不足，集成方法虽被用于增强鲁棒性，但如何在连续上下文下保持计算可行性的分布鲁棒贝叶斯优化仍是一个挑战。

Method: 提出了一种集成分布鲁棒贝叶斯优化算法，利用集成模型作为替代模型，在连续上下文条件下保持计算可处理性。

Result: 获得了理论上的亚线性遗憾界，改进了现有的最优结果；实验行为与理论保证一致。

Conclusion: 该算法在理论和实践层面均有效，为连续上下文下的分布鲁棒贝叶斯优化提供了新的可行方案。

Abstract: We study zeroth-order optimisation under context distributional uncertainty, a setting commonly tackled using Bayesian optimisation (BO). A prevailing strategy to make BO more robust to the complex and noisy nature of data is to employ an ensemble as the surrogate model, thereby mitigating the weaknesses of any single model. In this study, we propose a novel algorithm for Ensemble Distributionally Robust Bayesian Optimisation that remains computationally tractable while managing continuous context. We obtain theoretical sublinear regret bounds, improving current state-of-the-art results. We show that our method's empirical behaviour aligns with its theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [209] [Approximation Error Upper and Lower Bounds for Hölder Class with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07463)
*Xin He,Yuling Jiao,Xiliang Lu,Jerry Zhijian Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer对Hölder函数的表达能力，给出了近似误差的上界和下界，证明了需要$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-d_0/\alpha})$个块实现$\varepsilon$精度，下界至少$\Omega(\varepsilon^{-d_0/(4\alpha)})$个块，并扩展到了回归任务的风险分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Transformer架构对Hölder类函数的表达能力，从理论上精确刻画其近似误差的上下界，理解其强大性能的理论根源。

Method: 针对标准Transformer架构（含Softmax、ReLU和残差连接），推导了逼近Hölder函数的上界；利用VC维上界首次严格证明了逼近下界；并将结果推广到一般回归任务中的超额风险分析。

Result: 上界：最多$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-d_0/\alpha})$个块即可逼近任意有界Hölder函数；下界：至少需要$\Omega(\varepsilon^{-d_0/(4\alpha)})$个块才能达到$\varepsilon$精度。

Conclusion: 从理论上证明了Transformer对光滑函数的表达能力，上下界结果揭示了其逼近效率与输入维度和光滑度的依赖关系，回归任务的风险分析验证了其实践有效性。

Abstract: We explore the expressive power of Transformers by establishing precise approximation error upper and lower bounds for Hölder class. Specifically, a new approximation upper bound is derived for the standard Transformer architecture equipped with Softmax operators, ReLU activation functions, and residual connections. We prove that a Transformer network composed of at most $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-{d_{0}}/α})$ blocks can approximate any bounded Hölder function with $d_{0}$-dimensional input and smoothness $α\in(0,1]$ under any accuracy $\varepsilon>0$. In the case of approximation lower bounds, leveraging the VC-dimension upper bound, we are the first to rigorously prove that Transformers demand for at least $Ω(\varepsilon^{-{d_{0}}/({4α})})$ blocks to achieve the $\varepsilon$ approximation accuracy. As a final step, we extend the derived results for standard Transformers to a general regression task and establish the corresponding excess risk rates demonstrating Transformers' empirical effectiveness in real-world settings.

</details>


### [210] [Revisiting Transformer Layer Parameterization Through Causal Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07588)
*Jin Xu,Camille Couturier,Victor Rühle,Saravan Rajmohan,James Hensman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出因果能量最小化（CEM）框架，将Transformer层重新解释为条件能量函数的优化步骤，表明权重绑定的多头注意力可视为交互能量的梯度更新，门控MLP可通过元素级能量理解，并识别出包含层内权重共享、对角加低秩交互等设计空间。在亿级参数语言模型实验中，CEM衍生层虽参数受限，但训练稳定且性能匹配基线Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer块中多头注意力和门控MLP的许多参数化选择仍然是经验性的，缺乏统一的理论理解，作者希望从能量最小化视角提供更系统的理论框架。

Method: 提出CEM框架，将Transformer层重构为条件能量函数的优化步骤：权重绑定MHA推导为交互能量的梯度更新，门控MLP通过元素级能量视角解释，并探索了层内权重共享、对角加低秩交互、轻量预条件子和递归更新等设计空间。

Result: 在亿级参数规模的语言建模实验中，CEM衍生层训练稳定，且能匹配对应Transformer基线的性能。

Conclusion: CEM为理解Transformer层参数化提供了有用视角，将Transformer架构与基于能量的模型联系起来，并激励进一步探索能量引导的层设计。

Abstract: Transformer blocks typically combine multi-head attention (MHA) for token mixing with gated MLPs for token-wise feature transformation, yet many choices in their parameterization remain largely empirical. We introduce Causal Energy Minimization (CEM), a framework that recasts Transformer layers as optimization steps on conditional energy functions while explicitly accounting for layer parameterization. Extending prior energy-based interpretations of attention, CEM shows that weight-tied MHA can be derived as a gradient update on an interaction energy, and that a gated MLP with shared up/down projections can be viewed through an element-wise energy. This perspective identifies a design space for Transformer layers that includes within-layer weight sharing, diagonal-plus-low-rank interactions, lightweight preconditioners, and recursive updates. We evaluate CEM-derived layers in language-modeling experiments at the moderate hundred-million-parameter scale. Despite their constrained parameterizations, these layers train stably and can match corresponding Transformer baselines. Overall, our results suggest that CEM provides a useful lens for understanding Transformer layer parameterization, connecting Transformer architectures to energy-based models and motivating further exploration of energy-guided layer designs.

</details>


### [211] [NPMixer: Hierarchical Neighboring Patch Mixing for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07476)
*Jung Min Choi,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NPMixer，一种结合可学习平稳小波变换和邻域混合块的分层架构，用于多变量时间序列预测，在7个基准数据集上超越现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列预测因局部时间动态和全局跨变量依赖的复杂性而面临挑战，现有方法难以同时捕捉这两者。

Method: 提出NPMixer架构，包含可学习平稳小波变换（自适应学习滤波器系数、分解趋势与细节）、邻域混合块（分层MLP操作非重叠块、学习块内及块间时序模式）、通道混合编码器（应用于高频成分学习通道相关性）。

Result: 在7个基准数据集上，NPMixer在28个实验设置中的20个（71.4%）取得MSE最优性能，一致超越现有最先进模型。

Conclusion: NPMixer通过分层混合架构有效捕捉局部与全局依赖，验证了可学习小波变换在时间序列分解中的有效性。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting remains a challenge due to the complexity of local temporal dynamics and global dependencies across multiple variables.
  In this paper, we propose \textbf{N}eighboring \textbf{P}atching \textbf{Mixer} (\textbf{NPMixer}), a hierarchical architecture featuring a Learnable Stationary Wavelet Transform that adaptively learns filter coefficients to decompose signals into trend and detail components in a data-dependent manner.
  Our framework introduces a Neighboring Mixer Block that captures local temporal dynamics through a series of hierarchical MLP layers operating on non-overlapping patches.
  Specifically, the mixer block utilizes MLPs to learn temporal patterns within and across these patches, expanding the receptive field to capture multi-scale dependencies.
  A Channel-Mixing Encoder is applied to high-frequency components to learn channel correlations while preserving the stability of the underlying global trend.
  Extensive experiments on seven benchmark datasets demonstrate that NPMixer consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models, achieving better performance in 20 out of 28 ($71.4\%$) evaluated experimental setups for MSE.

</details>


### [212] [An Efficient Hybrid Sparse Attention with CPU-GPU Parallelism for Long-Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07719)
*Feiyu Yao,Zhixiong Niu,Xiaqing Li,Yongqiang Xiong,Juan Fang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fluxion是一种用于长上下文推理的混合稀疏注意力系统，通过输出感知的KV预算、头特定和粒度感知的稀疏配置以及跨设备协调执行，在CPU驻留KV缓存上实现了1.5-3.7倍加速，同时质量损失极小（平均仅-0.26）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中KV缓存常驻留CPU内存，现有GPU-only设计受限于PCIe带宽和元数据开销，CPU-GPU混合设计则存在GPU空闲和CPU端top-k选择/稀疏计算瓶颈，需要更高效的协同方案。

Method: Fluxion包含三个关键组件：轻量级头属性预测器、粒度预算选择器和基于优先级的调度器，共同优化预算分配、稀疏配置和CPU-GPU执行重叠，实现混合稀疏注意力的端到端协同设计。

Result: 在2个模型、3个基准测试和40个任务上，Fluxion相比最强固定稀疏混合基线（KV预算仅0.05）实现1.5-3.7倍加速，最差平均质量下降仅为-0.26。

Conclusion: Fluxion证明了输出感知预算、头特定稀疏配置和跨设备协调执行可以同时实现长上下文推理的准确性和系统效率。

Abstract: Long-context inference increasingly operates over CPU-resident KV caches, either because decoding-time KV states exceed GPU memory capacity or because disaggregated prefill-decode systems place KV data in host memory. Although block-sparse attention reduces attention cost in this setting, sparsity alone is insufficient for end-to-end efficiency. GPU-only designs remain constrained by PCIe bandwidth and metadata memory overhead, while CPU-GPU hybrid designs still suffer from substantial GPU idle time and bottlenecks in CPU-side top-k selection and sparse attention computation.
  Fluxion is built on three key insights: output-aware KV budgeting, head-specific and granularity-aware sparse configuration, and cross-device coordinated execution for sparse attention over CPU-resident KV caches. Guided by these insights, Fluxion combines a lightweight head-property predictor, a granularity-budget selector, and a priority-based scheduler to jointly optimize budget allocation, sparse configuration, and CPU-GPU execution overlap. This co-design enables hybrid sparse attention to achieve both accuracy and system efficiency in long-context inference. Across 2 models, 3 benchmarks, and 40 tasks, Fluxion preserves quality well -- the worst average degradation is only -0.26 relative to FULL, while delivering 1.5$\times$-3.7$\times$ speedup over the strongest fixed sparse hybrid baseline, whose KV budget is only 0.05.

</details>


### [213] [Curated Synthetic Data Doesn't Have to Collapse: A Theoretical Study of Generative Retraining with Pluralistic Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07724)
*Ali Falahati,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Kate Larson,Lukasz Golab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过多奖励函数进行数据筛选，可避免递归训练生成模型时的模式坍缩，模型收敛到加权纳什协商解，保持输出多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归训练生成模型时，基于单一奖励筛选合成数据会导致模型坍缩到狭窄的高奖励区域，先前工作认为除非加入真实数据否则无法避免。作者从对齐角度重新审视这一问题。

Method: 形式化分析了异构偏好下递归训练的动态过程，证明了在多种奖励函数条件下，模型收敛到在竞争性高奖励区域间分配概率质量的稳定分布。

Result: 多奖励筛选可缓解模式坍缩，收敛分布保持多样性，且满足加权纳什协商解。

Conclusion: 为合成数据递归训练中的价值聚合提供了形式化解释，表明多奖励对齐策略是避免坍缩的有效途径。

Abstract: Recursive retraining of generative models poses a critical representation challenge: when synthetic outputs are curated based on a fixed reward signal, the model tends to collapse onto a narrow set of outputs that over-optimize that objective. Prior work suggests that such collapse is unavoidable without adding real data into the mix. We revisit this conclusion from an alignment perspective and show that collapse can be mitigated through curation based on multiple reward functions. We formalize the dynamics of recursive training under heterogeneous preferences and prove that, under certain conditions, the model converges to a stable distribution that allocates probability mass across competing high-reward regions. The limiting distribution preserves diversity and provably satisfies a weighted Nash bargaining solution, offering a formal interpretation of value aggregation in synthetic retraining loops.

</details>


### [214] [SGD for Variational Inference: Tackling Unbounded Variance via Preconditioning and Dynamic Batching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07531)
*Hippolyte Labarrière,Cesare Molinari,Silvia Villa,Lorenzo Rosasco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了黑盒变分推理（BBVI）中随机梯度的无界方差问题，在椭圆位置-尺度分布族下证明了ELBO解的存在性，并为配备动态批处理和预条件的小批量投影SGD算法在Blum-Gladyshev条件下建立了完整的收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BBVI中随机梯度存在固有的无界方差，违反标准SGD假设，仅满足较弱的Blum-Gladyshev条件。现有理论分析与实际应用之间存在差距，需要更严谨的收敛理论来指导实践。

Method: 聚焦椭圆位置-尺度分布族，证明ELBO解的存在性；采用配备动态批处理和预条件的小批量投影SGD（PSGD）算法，并在BG条件下建立有限时间和渐近收敛保证。

Result: 在BG条件下，动态批处理结合预条件策略能够系统性地为复杂场景提供严格的收敛保证；数值实验验证了理论发现的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了随机优化理论与BBVI实践之间的鸿沟，为现代推理任务提供了理论支撑和实用方法。

Abstract: Black-Box Variational Inference (BBVI) typically relies on Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) to optimize the Evidence Lower Bound (ELBO). However, the stochastic gradients in BBVI inherently exhibit unbounded variance, violating standard assumptions and instead satisfying the weaker Blum-Gladyshev (BG) condition, where variance grows quadratically with distance from the optimum. In this paper, we bridge the gap between stochastic optimization theory and the practical instances of BBVI. Focusing on the broad elliptic location-scale family of parameterized distributions, we offer two main contributions. First, we prove the existence of an ELBO solution, a foundational property usually assumed a priori in the literature. Second, we establish comprehensive convergence guarantees spanning finite-time and asymptotic regimes for Minibatch Projected SGD (PSGD) equipped with dynamic batching and preconditioning under the BG condition. Our theoretical framework demonstrates that dynamic batching combined with preconditioning systematically enables rigorous guarantees even in complex settings. We illustrate our theoretical findings with numerical results, highlighting the efficacy of our approach for modern inference tasks.

</details>


### [215] [On the Invariance and Generality of Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07546)
*Xing Han,Ziyin Liu,Suchi Saria,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可泛化的缩放定律框架，通过信息分辨率的单轴变换，使得在一个领域拟合的缩放定律能够可靠地迁移到另一个领域，验证了跨语言、视觉、语音及电子健康记录等应用的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的缩放定律需要针对每个新任务-模型配对进行昂贵的数据扫描，耗费了大量计算资源。本文旨在开发可泛化的缩放定律，使其从一个资源充足的源域迁移到新领域，从而节省计算成本。

Method: 识别了缩放定律在双射（信息保持）变换下保持不变的特性，并在非双射（信息降低）变换下通过信息分辨率 ρ 这一单一轴进行可预测的调整，从而实现跨域迁移。

Result: 在语言、视觉和语音三个模态上验证了方法的有效性；在电子健康记录语言模型缩放预测和噪声注入下的时间序列分类任务中，数据缩放指数的误差控制在3%以内。

Conclusion: 缩放定律在信息分辨率视角下具有跨域迁移性，能够显著降低新领域拟合缩放定律的计算成本，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws establish a predictable relationship between model performance and data or compute, offering crucial guidance for resource allocation in new domains and tasks. Yet such laws are most needed precisely where they are hardest to obtain: fitting one for a new model task pair demands expensive sweeps that typically exhaust the very compute budget the law is meant to economize. This paper poses the research question of how to develop generalizable scaling laws: laws fit once on a well-resourced source domain and reliably transported to new domains where running a full sweep is infeasible, which requires a fundamental understanding of when and why scaling properties change. We address this by identifying the right invariants: scaling laws are preserved under bijective (information-preserving) transformations of the data and modified in predictable, information-theoretically grounded ways under non-bijective transformations that lower its information resolution $ρ$: a single axis along which a law fit in one domain can be transported to another. We validate this across language, vision, and speech, and demonstrate two cross-domain applications: predicting scaling for language models trained on electronic health records from laws fit on general text, and predicting time-series classification scaling under varying levels of noise injection, recovering the data-scaling exponents to within $3\%$ error.

</details>


### [216] [Drifting Field Policy: A One-Step Generative Policy via Wasserstein Gradient Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07727)
*Juil Koo,Mingue Park,Jiwon Choi,Yunhong Min,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于漂移模型范式的非ODE一步生成策略DFP，通过将策略更新视为概率空间中的梯度流，实现了在多个操作任务上的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于ODE的生成策略存在推理速度慢的问题，且传统策略更新方法缺乏对概率空间中梯度流的理论框架。作者希望设计一种非ODE、一步生成的策略，同时保持理论严谨性和实际性能。

Method: 将策略更新建模为反向KL Wasserstein-2梯度流，向软目标策略逼近。梯度分解为两部分：向高动作值区域上升的方向，以及与锚点策略进行分数匹配的信任区域。最终推导出可操作的替代损失函数，类似于对 critic 选择的 top-K 动作进行行为克隆。

Result: 在Robomimic和OGBench的多个操作任务上，DFP在一步推断下达到了最先进性能，优于基于ODE的策略。

Conclusion: DFP验证了非ODE生成范式的有效性，为强化学习中的策略优化提供了新的理论框架和实用方法。

Abstract: We propose Drifting Field Policy (DFP), a non-ODE one-step generative policy built on the drifting model paradigm. We frame the policy update as a reverse-KL Wasserstein-2 gradient flow toward a soft target policy, so that each DFP update corresponds to a gradient step in probability space. By construction, this gradient is decomposed into an ascent toward higher action-value regions and a score matching with the anchor policy as a trust region. We further derive a simple, tractable surrogate of the otherwise intractable update loss, akin to behavior cloning on top-K critic-selected actions. We find empirically that this mechanism uniquely benefits the drifting backbone owing to its non-ODE parameterization. With one-step inference, DFP achieves state-of-the-art performance on several manipulation tasks across Robomimic and OGBench, outperforming ODE-based policies.

</details>


### [217] [Intelligent Truck Matching in Full Truckload Shipments using Ping2Hex approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07733)
*Srinivas Kumar R,Jose Mathew,Ankit Singh Chauhan,Dinesh Rajkumar,Aravind Manoj,Mohit Goel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ITM 2.0系统，利用GPS数据通过机器学习（LightGBM）将卡车与货物运输匹配建模为概率排序问题，在北美和欧洲分别提升26和14个百分点精度，覆盖范围翻倍，已部署于Project44生产环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆标识符缺失或损坏导致传统匹配方法失效，使得货物运输失去可见性，无法实现实时追踪和准确的ETA预测。

Method: 将匹配问题建模为概率排序，使用Uber H3六边形空间索引离散化GPS点以提取路径相似性特征，结合时间信息，采用LightGBM梯度提升模型及阈值后处理。

Result: 北美精度提升26个百分点，欧洲提升14个百分点，覆盖范围翻倍；在生产环境中对地理编码误差（1公里以内）、多个候选车辆和稀疏定位点均表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ITM 2.0有效解决了标识符缺失情况下的卡车-货物匹配问题，在真实生产环境中取得了显著性能提升，为全卡车运输供应链可视性提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Accurate truck-to-shipment matching using GPS data is foundational for full truckload supply chain visibility, enabling real-time tracking and accurate estimated time of arrival (ETA) predictions. However, missing or corrupted vehicle identifiers prevent traditional matching approaches, leaving shipments without visibility. This paper presents Intelligent Truck Matching (ITM) 2.0, a machine learning system that addresses this critical gap by formulating matching as a probabilistic ranking problem. Our approach leverages Uber H3 hexagonal spatial indexing to discretize GPS pings into route similarity features, combined with temporal information, then applies LightGBM gradient boosting with threshold-based post-processing. Through rigorous evaluation including offline model selection (SVM, XGBoost, LightGBM), comprehensive ablation studies, and production shadow testing, we demonstrate substantial gains over rule-based baselines. ITM 2.0 achieves 26 percentage point precision improvement in North America and 14 points in Europe, while doubling coverage. Deployed in production at Project44 handling full truckload shipments, the system demonstrates robustness to geocoding errors up to 1 km, multiple candidate trucks, and sparse pings.

</details>


### [218] [Beyond Distribution Estimation: Simplex Anchored Structural Inference Towards Universal Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07557)
*Yaxin Hou,Jun Ma,Hanyang Li,Bo Han,Jie Yu,Yuheng Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对现实场景中标注数据稀缺且未标注数据分布未知的通用半监督学习（UniSSL）问题，提出了一种基于表示级结构推理的Simplex Anchored Graph-state Equipartition（SAGE）方法，通过捕获高阶样本间依赖关系建立结构共识、利用单纯形等角紧框架引导类间表示分离，并在五个标准基准上平均提升8.52%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督学习方法通常假设未标注数据服从均匀分布，或需要充足的标注数据来估计分布，但在实际UniSSL场景中，未标注数据分布是未知且任意的，这导致伪标签错误率极高，引发表示混淆（representation confusion）。本文旨在解决这一尚未充分探索的挑战。

Method: 提出SAGE方法：1）利用样本间表示关系比伪标签更可靠的观察，转向表示级结构推理，通过Simplex Anchored Graph-state Equipartition捕获高阶样本间依赖关系，建立结构共识指导表示学习；2）采用满足单纯形等角紧框架（simplex equiangular tight frame）的向量作为坐标框架，引导类间表示分离，缓解表示混淆；3）引入基于分布无关度量的加权策略优先选择可靠伪标签，并设计辅助分支隔离潜在的错误伪标签。

Result: 在五个标准基准数据集上的评估表明，SAGE方法一致性地优于现有最先进方法，平均准确率提升8.52%。

Conclusion: 通过表示级结构推理绕过分布估计，结合单纯形等角紧框架和可靠的伪标签加权策略，有效解决了通用半监督学习中的表示混淆问题，显著提升了模型在复杂真实场景下的性能。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning faces significant challenges in realistic scenarios where labeled data is scarce and unlabeled data follows unknown, arbitrary distributions. We formalize this critical yet under-explored paradigm as Universal Semi-supervised Learning (UniSSL). Existing methods typically leverage unlabeled data via pseudo-labeling. However, they often rely on the idealized assumption of a uniform unlabeled data distribution or require sufficient labeled data to estimate it. In the UniSSL setting, such dependencies lead to numerous erroneous pseudo-labels, thereby triggering representation confusion. Fortunately, we observe that inter-sample relations captured by representations are more reliable than pseudo-labels. Leveraging this insight, we shift our focus to representation-level structural inference to bypass distribution estimation. Accordingly, we propose Simplex Anchored Graph-state Equipartition (SAGE), which captures high-order inter-sample dependencies to establish structural consensus for guiding representation learning. Meanwhile, to mitigate representation confusion, we employ vectors that satisfy a simplex equiangular tight frame to serve as a coordinate frame for guiding inter-class representation separation. Finally, we introduce a weighting strategy based on distribution-agnostic metrics to prioritize reliable pseudo-labels and an auxiliary branch to isolate potentially erroneous pseudo-labels. Evaluations on five standard benchmarks show that SAGE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, with an average accuracy gain of \textbf{8.52\%}.

</details>


### [219] [When Losses Align: Gradient-Based Composite Loss Weighting for Efficient Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07756)
*Ivan Karpukhin,Andrey Savchenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于梯度的双层优化方法，在线调整预训练损失权重，使复合梯度与下游目标对齐，在事件序列建模和自监督视觉任务上匹配或超越精心调优的基线，且超参数调优成本仅为单次训练的约30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度模型常使用复合目标函数在缺失标签的大规模数据上预训练，但多个损失项的权重作为超参数，传统调优方法（随机搜索或贝叶斯优化）需要大量独立训练运行，计算成本过高。

Method: 提出梯度驱动的双层优化方法，在线学习预训练损失权重，通过将复合预训练梯度与下游目标对齐来更新权重，利用损失结构避免多次反向传播，将开销降低到单次训练的约30%。

Result: 在事件序列建模和自监督计算机视觉任务上，该方法匹配或超越精心调优的基线，同时显著降低超参数调优成本。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效降低了预训练损失权重调优的计算负担，实现高效且性能不妥协的超参数学习。

Abstract: Modern deep models are often pretrained on large-scale data with missing labels using composite objectives, where the relative weights of multiple loss terms act as hyperparameters. Tuning these weights with random search or Bayesian optimization is computationally expensive, as it requires many independent training runs. To address this, we propose a gradient-based bilevel method that learns pretraining loss weights online by aligning the composite pretraining gradient with a downstream objective. By exploiting the structure of the loss, the method avoids the multiple backward passes typically required by truncated backpropagation through the full model, reducing the overhead of hyperparameter tuning to approximately 30% above a single training run. We evaluate the approach on event-sequence modeling and self-supervised computer vision, where it matches or improves upon carefully tuned baselines while substantially reducing the cost of hyperparameter tuning compared to random or Bayesian search.

</details>


### [220] [Bilevel Graph Structure Learning, Revisited: Inner-Channel Origins of the Reported Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07577)
*Minkyoung Kim,Beakcheol Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现双层图结构学习的性能提升主要来自内循环训练动态（隐式梯度正则化），而非图结构重连本身，并提出了frozen-φ诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将双层图结构学习的性能增益归因于重连后的图结构，但作者怀疑这一归因可能被夸大，希望厘清真实贡献来源。

Method: 提出frozen-φ控制方法，冻结图结构但保留内循环训练调度，将双层增益分解为内通道（T步训练动态+隐式梯度正则化）和图通道（图结构重连）。

Result: 时空流预测中内通道贡献78-101%的增益，节点分类中贡献37-44%；经典谱诊断指标与任务增益可能不相关。

Conclusion: 建议将frozen-φ作为双层图结构学习的标准化诊断工具，并提出三 precondition 框架可预测所有六个基准上的双层增益方向。

Abstract: Bilevel graph structure learning is widely understood to improve graph neural networks by jointly optimizing model parameters and a learned graph structure, with the resulting performance gain attributed to the rewired adjacency. We find that this attribution may be overstated: training-dynamics effects in the inner loop, rather than the rewiring itself, capture a substantial share of the gain. To establish this, we introduce frozen-$φ$, a control that freezes the graph while retaining the inner-loop training schedule. This decomposes the bilevel gain into an inner channel of $T$-step training dynamics with implicit gradient regularization and a graph channel of the graph rewiring itself. On spatio-temporal flow forecasting the inner channel matches or exceeds the full bilevel pipeline, accounting for 78-101% of the gain; on node classification it accounts for 37-44% under a Bernoulli edge-level parameterization. We also verify that classical spectral diagnostics can dissociate from task gain. We propose frozen-$φ$ as a standardized diagnostic for bilevel graph structure learning, with graph distillation as a method-agnostic complement. A three-precondition framework further predicts the sign of the bilevel gain on all six benchmarks.

</details>


### [221] [Toward Privileged Foundation Models:LUPI for Accelerated and Improved Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07799)
*Xueying Ding,Leman Akoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PIQL是一个系统性地利用特权信息（PI）来加速表格基础模型（TFMs）学习和提升泛化能力的框架，通过聚合数据集统计信息和底层数据生成程序编码两种PI形式，并设计能推理时重构PI的架构，在理论和实验上均证明了其降低计算成本、加速收敛和提升泛化效果的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练基础模型计算量大且收敛速度慢，现有方法缺乏系统性地利用特权信息来同时加速学习和提升泛化能力的手段。

Method: 设计两种互补的特权信息：聚合数据集级别统计量和底层数据生成程序编码；构建能训练时利用PI、推理时从观测上下文重构PI的架构。

Result: PIQL实现了更快的收敛速度、更低的最终损失和更好的泛化性能，有效降低了数据和计算需求。

Conclusion: PI引导的预训练是一种提升基础模型效率和性能的既原则性又实用的新范式。

Abstract: Training foundation models is computationally intensive and often slow to converge.We introduce PIQL,Privileged Information for Quick and Quality Learning, the first framework to systematically integrate privileged information (PI) to simultaneously accelerate learning and improve generalization in tabular foundation models (TFMs). We construct two complementary forms of PI: (i) aggregate dataset-level statistics that reduce the burden on in-context learning, and (ii) encodings of the underlying data-generating program, providing knowledge beyond observable data. We further design an architecture that effectively transfers the train-time-only PI by learning to reconstruct it from observed context at inference. We provide a theoretical analysis characterizing conditions under which PI reduces the population-level approximation gap and accelerates convergence in finite-data regimes. Empirical evidence shows that PIQL enables TFMs to achieve faster convergence, lower final loss, and better generalization, in effect, reducing data and compute requirements. Our work establishes PI-guided pretraining as a principled and practical paradigm for improving the efficiency and performance of foundation models.

</details>


### [222] [Optimal Recourse Summaries via Bi-Objective Decision Tree Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07598)
*Ioannis Chatzis,Jason Liartis,Athanasios Voulodimos,Giorgos Stamou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SOGAR方法，通过最优决策树学习生成全局可操作补救方案总结，在补救效果与成本之间实现帕累托最优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可操作补救方法局限于个体层面，难以用于全局审计和偏差检测，且聚合个体行动成本高、不一致；同时缺乏有效处理补救效果与成本之间权衡的方法。

Method: 将补救方案总结学习建模为最优决策树学习问题，寻找帕累托前沿（效果与成本的双目标优化），使用浅层轴平行决策树和稀疏叶节点行动生成总结。

Result: SOGAR生成稳定、低成本且有效的补救方案总结，在效果和成本指标上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SOGAR支持事后选择期望的权衡而不需重新训练，为全局审计和偏差检测提供了有效的工具。

Abstract: Actionable Recourse provides individuals with actions they can take to change an unfavorable classifier outcome. While useful at the instance level, it is ill-suited for global auditing and bias detection, since aggregating local actions is costly and often inconsistent. Recourse Summaries address this limitation by partitioning the population and assigning one shared action per subgroup, enabling comparison across subgroups. Designing summaries involves a fundamental trade-off between recourse effectiveness and recourse cost, which existing methods do not adequately address. We introduce Summaries of Optimal and Global Actionable Recourse (SOGAR), which formulates recourse summary learning as an optimal decision tree learning problem and finds the Pareto front -- the complete set of solutions where improving one objective necessarily worsens the other. SOGAR enables post-hoc selection of the desired trade-off without retraining. Using shallow axis-parallel decision trees and sparse leaf actions, SOGAR produces stable, low-cost, and effective recourse summaries that outperform existing approaches across effectiveness and cost metrics.

</details>


### [223] [Prune-OPD: Efficient and Reliable On-Policy Distillation for Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07804)
*Zhicheng Yang,Zhijiang Guo,Yifan Song,Minrui Xu,Yongxin Wang,Yiwei Wang,Xiaodan Liang,Jing Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Prune-OPD通过动态检测学生与教师之间的前缀漂移，实时截断无效rollout并重新分配计算资源，在减少37.6%-68.0%训练时间的同时保持甚至提升推理模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy distillation (OPD)在长程任务中面临关键缺陷：学生生成前缀不可避免地偏离教师思路，导致教师密集奖励失去局部可剥削性，继续生成和评估token不仅降低奖励质量，还造成大量计算浪费。

Method: 提出Prune-OPD框架，通过持续监控学生与教师预测间的局部兼容性（如top-k重叠）实时检测前缀漂移事件，一旦发现严重漂移，单调降低后续不可靠奖励权重并触发动态rollout截断，将计算重新分配给可靠的教师监督。

Result: 在多种教师-学生组合下，当前缀漂移使密集教师奖励不可靠时，减少37.6%-68.0%训练时间，同时在AMC、AIME、HMMT等挑战性基准上保持或提升性能；当兼容性高时，自动延长训练窗口保留长上下文监督。

Conclusion: Prune-OPD改进OPD的方式不是盲目缩短rollout，而是将计算重新分配给局部可剥削的教师奖励，实现计算与监督可靠性的自适应对齐。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) leverages dense teacher rewards to enhance reasoning models. However, scaling OPD to long-horizon tasks exposes a critical flaw: as the student's generated prefix inevitably diverges from the teacher's thought process, the teacher's dense reward loses local exploitability. Continuing to generate and evaluate tokens on these ``drifted'' trajectories not only degrades reward quality but also incurs massive computational waste. To address this, we introduce \textbf{Prune-OPD}, a framework that dynamically aligns training budgets with supervision quality. By continuously monitoring the local compatibility between student and teacher predictions (e.g., via top-$k$ overlap), Prune-OPD detects prefix-drift events in real time. Upon detecting severe drift, it monotonically down-weights subsequent unreliable rewards and triggers dynamic rollout truncation. This allows the training process to halt futile generation and reallocate compute strictly to reliable teacher supervision. Across diverse teacher-student combinations, Prune-OPD consistently aligns computation with supervision reliability. When prefix drift makes dense teacher rewards unreliable, it reduces training time by 37.6\%--68.0\% while preserving, and often improving, performance on challenging benchmarks (AMC, AIME, HMMT). When student-teacher compatibility remains high, it automatically preserves long-context supervision by expanding the training window. These results suggest that Prune-OPD improves OPD not by blindly shortening rollouts, but by reallocating computation toward locally exploitable teacher rewards.

</details>


### [224] [Learning Large-Scale Modular Addition with an Auxiliary Modulus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07648)
*Hanato Kikuchi,Ryosuke Masuya,Kazuhiko Kawamoto,Hiroshi Kera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对模加法学习中的协变量偏移问题，提出了一种无协变量偏移的改进方法，通过引入辅助模数Kq在训练阶段减少环绕频率，同时保持训练与测试分布一致，在长序列、大模数、小样本场景下显著优于现有稀疏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏方法虽通过增加训练序列中零的比例降低了模加法学习难度，但导致了训练与测试输入分布不一致的协变量偏移问题，影响模型泛化能力。

Method: 在训练阶段引入一个辅助模数Kq（K > 1），使加法结果在更大的模数空间内分布，减少环绕（wrap-around）概率，从而降低问题难度；同时保持训练与测试时的输入分布一致，避免协变量偏移。

Result: 在N=64、q=974269的大规模设定下，所提方法使用10万样本即可达到97.0%的τ-准确率（τ=0.05），而稀疏方法在相同数据量下仅9.5%，即使扩大到100万样本也仅为93.9%。

Conclusion: 所提无协变量偏移方法在长序列、大模数、小样本场景下具有更强的可扩展性和样本效率，有效解决了稀疏方法中分布偏移带来的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Learning parity functions, more general modular addition, is a challenging machine learning task due to its input sensitivity. A recent study substantially scaled modular addition learning in both the number of summands and the modulus. Its key idea is to increase zeros in training sequences, reducing the effective number of summands and thus controlling training difficulty; however, this induces covariate shift between training and test input distributions. This study theoretically and empirically analyzes this side effect and proposes a covariate-shift-free method for modular addition. Specifically, we introduce an auxiliary modulus $Kq$ during training, which reduces wrap-around frequency and problem difficulty while preserving the same input distribution across training and testing. Experiments show strong scalability and sample efficiency: even for large input length $N$, large modulus $q$, and small datasets -- where the sparse method fails to learn -- our method achieves equal or better match accuracy and relaxed $τ$-accuracy. For example, at $N=64$ and $q=974269$, our method trained on 100K samples achieves $97.0\%$ $τ$-accuracy at $τ=0.05$, while the sparse method achieves only $9.5\%$ with the same data size and $93.9\%$ even when extended to 1M samples.

</details>


### [225] [Stochastic Transition-Map Distillation for Fast Probabilistic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07661)
*George Rapakoulias,Peter Garud,Lingjiong Zhu,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STMD（随机转移图蒸馏），一种无需教师模型、无需双层优化或轨迹缓存的扩散模型加速推理框架，通过蒸馏SDE转移映射实现一步或少步随机采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽生成质量高，但推理成本昂贵，现有蒸馏方法依赖预训练教师模型、双层优化或轨迹缓存，效率较低。

Method: 提出无教师框架STMD，蒸馏采样SDE的完整转移映射，并使用条件均值流模型参数化SDE转移，得到一步或少步随机采样器。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10、CelebA数据集上验证了图像生成效果，并推导了Wasserstein距离下的收敛界。

Conclusion: STMD无需教师模型、双层优化和轨迹缓存，训练高效可扩展，保留随机采样结构，适用于扩散后验采样、逆问题、能量微调等下游任务。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve strong generation quality, diversity, and distribution coverage, but their performance often comes with expensive inference. In this work, we propose Stochastic Transition-Map Distillation (STMD), a teacher-free framework for accelerating diffusion model inference while preserving probabilistic sample generation. In contrast to score-based diffusion models, whose denoising parametrization models the mean of the posterior distribution, STMD distills the full transition map associated with the sampling stochastic differential equation (SDE). We parameterize these SDE transitions with a conditional Mean Flow model, yielding a one- or few-step stochastic sampler that retains the transition structure of the underlying diffusion process. This perspective is especially useful for downstream tasks that require stochastic inference, such as diffusion posterior sampling, inverse problems, and energy-based fine-tuning. Compared to recent distillation methods, STMD requires no pretrained teacher, bi-level optimization, or trajectory simulation and caching, enabling efficient and scalable training. We derive convergence bounds for our method in the Wasserstein distance, providing a strong theoretical foundation for our approach, and validate STMD on various image generation examples on the MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CelebA datasets.

</details>


### [226] [Direction-Preserving Number Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07662)
*Bardia Zadeh,George A. Constantinides*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从几何角度分析了低精度数值格式（如定点数、浮点数）在表示向量方向时的效率，证明了这些标准格式在方向覆盖上存在理论次优性，并给出了量化差距。通过优化标量字母表，发现NVIDIA的NVFP4（E2M1）格式接近最优，为其在机器学习中的优越性能提供了几何解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度数值格式在机器学习中广泛使用，但现有标准格式（如补码、定点数、浮点数）对向量方向的表示能力缺乏严格的理论分析。本文旨在从几何框架出发，量化产品结构编码与球面编码之间的方向覆盖差距，并探索更优的标量字母表设计。

Method: 提出几何框架分析产品结构编码（各分量从有限字母表选取）的方向覆盖；理论量化产品编码与球面编码的次优性差距（低维和高维渐近情况）；证明补码、定点数、浮点数在同类编码中的次优性并给出量化差距；在多块维度上数值优化标量字母表，并与NVIDIA的NVFP4格式对比。

Result: 对于4比特，NVIDIA的E2M1格式非常接近优化后的字母表；标准格式均被证明是次优的，差距已被量化；提供了优化的字母表以及Lean形式化证明和实验代码。

Conclusion: 产品结构编码与球面编码存在可量化的方向覆盖差距，标准数值格式存在改进空间。NVIDIA的NVFP4格式在几何意义上接近最优，解释了其在低精度机器学习中的优异表现。本文为设计新标量数值格式提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Low-precision number formats are widely used in modern machine learning systems due to their efficiency. Accurate direction representation is key to the accuracy of vector operations. This work precisely explores the extent to which the direction of a vector can be represented by selecting its scalar elements from a common finite alphabet of a given size. This is standard practice in machine learning, where low-precision significands may be narrow-width floating-point or integer values. A geometric framework is introduced for analyzing the directional coverage of such product-structured codes.
  This work analytically quantifies the suboptimality gap between such product-structured codes and spherical codes for the vector as a whole, in both low and asymptotically high dimensions. Furthermore, within the product code class, it is proven that the standard formats of two's complement, fixed-point, and floating-point are suboptimal, again with quantified gap, pointing to the potential to develop new scalar number formats.
  Such scalar alphabets are numerically optimized across multiple block dimensions for directional coverage, including the dimension used in NVIDIA's NVFP4 format. Experimental results are presented comparing the performance of standard formats and the optimized alphabet. We find that for four bits, NVIDIA's choice of E2M1 closely approximates the optimized alphabet, providing a geometric explanation for its strong performance in low-precision machine learning workloads and an analytical understanding of the link between that superiority and block size.
  We provide open-source formal proofs in Lean for the theorems in this work, along with the experimental code and the optimized alphabets obtained.

</details>


### [227] [On the Tradeoffs of On-Device Generative Models in Federated Predictive Maintenance Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07860)
*Usevalad Milasheuski,Piero Baraldi,Enrico Zio,Stefano Savazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了VAE、GAN和扩散模型在联邦预测性维护中的性能与通信开销，提出了联邦生成模型的部分组件共享新分类法，实验揭示了模型效用、稳定性和可扩展性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在IoT分布式环境中保护客户端数据所有权，但现有研究多聚焦判别模型。生成模型（VAE、GAN、DM）在时序异常检测和预测性维护中潜力巨大，然而在联邦场景下的系统分析尚属空白，亟需探索其性能与通信开销的权衡。

Method: 提出联邦生成模型的新分类法，形式化部分组件共享作为模型个性化的原则性机制；在完整联邦与部分联邦（仅共享部分模型组件）两种设置下，对比VAE、GAN和扩散模型的性能与通信开销；在真实时序数据集上进行实验评估。

Result: GAN在完整联邦下训练稳定性优于独立本地训练，但不如VAE和DDPM鲁棒；扩散模型在带宽受限和非IID场景下，部分联邦（尤其仅共享解码器）可超越完整联邦的性能。

Conclusion: 不同生成模型在联邦预测性维护中存在显著的效用、稳定性和可扩展性权衡；部分组件共享为带宽受限和异构联邦场景提供了有效的个性化机制，其中扩散模型的解码器共享策略尤为突出。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for preserving client data ownership and control over distributed Internet of Things (IoT) environments. While discriminative models dominate most FL use cases, recent advances in generative models -- such as Variational Autoencoders (VAE), Generative Adversarial Networks (GAN), and Diffusion Models (DM) -- offer new opportunities for unsupervised anomaly detection in time series analysis, with relevant applications in predictive maintenance (PdM) in critical industrial infrastructures. In this work, we present a comprehensive analysis of VAEs, GANs, and DMs in the context of federated PdM. We analyze their performance and communication overhead under both full and partial federation setups, where only subsets of model components are shared. Building on this analysis, the paper proposes a novel taxonomy for federated generative models that formalizes partial component sharing as a principled mechanism for model personalization. Our experiments over a real-world time series dataset reveal distinct trade-offs in model utility, stability, and scalability, especially in heterogeneous and bandwidth-constrained FL settings. For the evaluated GAN-based configurations, full federation improves training stability relative to independent local training, although the model remains less robust than the VAE- and DDPM-based alternatives. For DMs, however, partial federation -- especially decoder sharing -- can outperform full federation in bandwidth-constrained, non-IID settings.

</details>


### [228] [Fortifying Time Series: DTW-Certified Robust Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07690)
*Shijie Liu,Tansu Alpcan,Christopher Leckie,Sarah Erfani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于DTW距离的时序异常检测认证鲁棒防御方法，通过随机平滑和范数转换技术，在DTW度量下提供可认证的鲁棒性保证，实验显示F1分数提升高达18.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序异常检测系统易受对抗攻击，但已有防御方法大多基于启发式或仅能在ℓ_p范数约束下提供认证鲁棒性，而ℓ_p范数无法捕捉时序数据的固有时间结构，导致小幅时间扭曲即可显著改变度量结果。DTW作为时序领域广泛采用的相似性度量，能处理时间对齐和时序变化，但此前尚无基于DTW度量的可认证鲁棒性结果。

Method: 通过将ℓ_p范数到DTW距离的下界变换进行桥接，将随机平滑范式适配到DTW度量空间，实现对时序异常检测模型的DTW认证鲁棒防御。

Result: 在多种数据集和模型上的实验验证了方法的有效性和实用性，在DTW对抗攻击下，相较于传统认证模型，F1分数最高提升18.7%。

Conclusion: 首次在DTW度量下实现了时序异常检测的可认证鲁棒性，填补了该领域的研究空白，为高安全关键应用中的时序系统提供了更可靠的防御保障。

Abstract: Time-series anomaly detection is critical for ensuring safety in high-stakes applications, where robustness is a fundamental requirement rather than a mere performance metric. Addressing the vulnerability of these systems to adversarial manipulation is therefore essential. Existing defenses are largely heuristic or provide certified robustness only under $\ell_p$-norm constraints, which are incompatible with time-series data. In particular, $\ell_p$-norm fails to capture the intrinsic temporal structure in time series, causing small temporal distortions to significantly alter the $\ell_p$-norm measures. Instead, the similarity metric \emph{Dynamic Time Warping} (DTW) is more suitable and widely adopted in the time-series domain, as DTW accounts for temporal alignment and remains robust to temporal variations. To date, however, there has been no certifiable robustness result in this metric that provides guarantees. In this work, we introduce the first \emph{DTW-certified robust defense} in time-series anomaly detection by adapting the randomized smoothing paradigm. We develop this certificate by bridging the $\ell_p$-norm to DTW distance through a lower-bound transformation. Extensive experiments across various datasets and models validate the effectiveness and practicality of our theoretical approach. Results demonstrate significantly improved performance, e.g., up to 18.7\% in F1-score under DTW-based adversarial attacks compared to traditional certified models.

</details>


### [229] [Toward Better Geometric Representations for Molecule Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07693)
*Shaoheng Yan,Zian Li,Cai Zhou,Qiaojing Huang,Kai Liu,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LENSEs框架，通过表示头、分子感知损失和节点级表示对齐三个机制，改善分子表示在条件生成中的平滑性和信息性，在GEOM-DRUG数据集上实现97.28%有效性和98.51%稳定性，超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示条件分子生成方法中，预训练分子编码器（如UniMol）产生的表示空间不平滑且未被生成训练过程充分利用，限制了生成质量和效率。

Method: 提出LENSEs框架，包含三个互补机制：(1)表示头，在生成任务中同步训练以提取多级表示；(2)分子感知损失，在语义信息丰富的表示空间中优化生成器；(3)节点级表示对齐(REPA)损失，将生成器隐藏状态与编码器表示对齐，减少预训练与生成之间的语义差距。
